```python
import os, glob
print("CWD:", os.getcwd())
print("Files in CWD:", os.listdir('.'))
for root, dirs, files in os.walk('.'):
    for f in files:
        print(os.path.join(root, f))

```python
import zipfile

zf = zipfile.ZipFile('./imshubhamsocial/ws-hermes', 'r')
names = zf.namelist()
print(f"Total entries in zip: {len(names)}")
prefix = names[0].split('/')[0] + '/'
print("Prefix:", prefix)

# Print all non-skill or top-level / important paths
rel_names = [n[len(prefix):] for n in names if n[len(prefix):]]
top_dirs = sorted(set(n.split('/')[0] for n in rel_names))
print("Top-level entries:", top_dirs)

for n in sorted(rel_names):
    if not n.startswith('.hermes/skills/'):
        print(n)


```

```python
import zipfile

zf = zipfile.ZipFile('./imshubhamsocial/ws-hermes', 'r')
prefix = zf.namelist()[0].split('/')[0] + '/'

gdr37_files = [
    'docs/research/GDR37/00_README.md',
    'docs/research/GDR37/01_master_argon_consultation_brief.md',

'docs/research/GDR37/02_doc1_physical_implementation_and_verification_report.md'
,

'docs/research/GDR37/03_doc2_jake_icm_commands_and_workspace_reorganization_spec
.md',
    'docs/research/GDR37/04_master_argon_prompt.md'
]

for f in gdr37_files:
    print("="*80)
    print("FILE:", f)
    print("="*80)
    print(zf.read(prefix + f).decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))


```

# GDR37 Master Release: Autonomous ICM Repository Organization & Verification
Blueprint

> **Release Package**: GDR37
> **Date**: November 6, 2026
> **Substrate Target**: Hermes Agent OS on Fedora Kinoite 44 (x86_64,
rpm-ostree)
> **Inference Substrate**: Gemma 4 Exact (`gemma-4-E4B-it-q4_0-exact.gguf` + MTP
Speculative Drafter)
> **Context Window Enforced**: 131,072 tokens (128K context, Google TurboQuant
Q4_0 KV Cache)
> **Hardware Status**: Discrete NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3050 Laptop GPU (6,144 MiB
GDDR6, VRAM active at 4,997 MiB, 861 MiB free headroom)
> **Status**: Phase 58 Verified & Sealed | 5-Layer ICM Architecture Deployed |

## 4. Mission Objectives for Lead Architect Argon

1. **Verify Implementation**: Review the physical deployment of Phase 58 and the
5-layer ICM structure.
2. **Execute Non-Breaking Safety Audit**: Enforce the Keep-Out Inventory to
protect daemons (Ports 8138, 1933, 8642, 9222), upstream purity
(`.hermes/hermes-agent`), and the 58 sealed phases.
3. **Classify All Repository Files**: Apply the 4-Category Triage framework
(Retain, Consolidate, Archive, Prune) to all repository files.

(`Source -> Destination | Rationale | Exact Git Command | Verification |
Rollback`).
5. **Output Standard**: Deliver instructions in strict ASD-STE100 format with
zero Google Docs export errors.

## 4. Benchmark Gauntlets Remediations

### 4.1 Gauntlet A: Voxel FPS Multiplayer

* **Problem**: Monolithic script failed to sync with the 20 Hz WebSocket tick.
* **Remediation**: Decomposed into 3 stages. Enforced strict 60-line script
limit. Bounded socket latency to under 50 milliseconds.

### 4.2 Gauntlet B: Splatbox OpenCV Ball Tracking

* **Problem**: Python process risked GPU memory fragmentation.
* **Remediation**: Deployed CPU `cv2.HoughCircles`. Verified 0 MiB GPU VRAM
consumption. Aiming accuracy within 5 pixels.

### 4.3 Gauntlet C: Three.js Aquarium Shader Material

* **Problem**: The browser logged:
```text
three.module.js:9206 THREE.Material: 'uniform' is not a property of
THREE.ShaderMaterial.

```


* **Remediation**: Replaced singular `uniform` property with dictionary
`uniforms: {}`. Verified clean WebGL rendering with zero console errors.

# Document 02: Jake Van Clief ICM Tooling & Workspace Reorganization
Specifications

> **Standard**: ASD-STE100 (Simplified Technical English)
> **Release Package**: GDR37
> **Theoretical Foundation**: Jake Van Clief & David McDermott
(arXiv:2603.16021)
> **Tool Sources**: `ktncodes/icm-template` and `RinDig/icm-architect`
> **Status**: Architectural Specification

---

## 1. Executive Summary

This document specifies the tooling and organizational principles of
Interpretable Context Methodology (ICM).
It explains how to apply Jake Van Clief's automated setup and synchronization
commands.
It provides the foundation to reorganize the `ws-hermes` workspace into a clean,
unified architecture.
It defines strict safety rules to guarantee that cleanup actions do not break
runtime systems.

---

## 2. Core ICM Principles

1. **One Stage, One Job**:
* Each folder executes a single, bounded contract.
* A stage that inspects state does not write code.
* A stage that calculates math does not dispatch actions.


2. **Plain Text as Universal Interface**:
* Stages communicate exclusively through markdown and JSON files.
* There are zero proprietary binary formats or closed databases between stages.


3. **Layered Context Loading**:
* Agents read downward through five layers and stop when they have what they
need.
* Layer 0: Where am I? (< 60 lines, ~800 tokens).
* Layer 1: Where do I go? (< 80 lines, ~300 tokens).
* Layer 2: What do I do? (< 80 lines, 200-500 tokens).
* Layer 3: What rules apply? (500-2,000 tokens).
* Layer 4: What am I working with? (Working deliverables).


4. **Every Output is an Edit Surface**:
* Humans can inspect, modify, and save any intermediate file before the next
stage runs.


5. **Configure the Factory, Not the Product**:
* Layer 3 rules and conventions stay constant across runs.
* Each execution produces clean Layer 4 deliverables using the existing factory.



---

## 3. Automated ICM Skills & Commands

The `ktncodes/icm-template` repository provides three primary agent skills:

| Command / Skill | Execution Purpose | Operating Behavior |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `/icm-scaffold` | Initial Workspace Setup | Analyzes workspace. Generates
`IDENTITY.md`, `CONTEXT.md`, and `_config/`. Supports **Quick Mode** (3 layers,
zero restructuring) and **Full Mode** (5 layers with stage folders). |
| `/icm-sync` | Routing Synchronization | Scans disk changes. Updates the
routing tables in `IDENTITY.md` and `CONTEXT.md` to match physical folders. |
| `/icm-context-scaffold` | Gap Remediation | Scans the directory tree.
Generates missing stage `CONTEXT.md` files with canonical contracts. |

---

## 4. ICM Architect Tooling Scripts

The `RinDig/icm-architect` skill contains deterministic Python validators:

* **`scripts/index.py`**: Builds the hierarchical context map across all layers.
* **`scripts/links.py`**: Verifies every relative Markdown file link across
stages.
* **`scripts/quotes.py`**: Verifies citation parity and ensures quotes match
source text.
* **`scripts/viz.py`**: Generates Mermaid sequence and flowchart diagrams of the
pipeline.

---

## 5. Unified Workspace Organization Model

To eliminate clutter and enforce **One Fact, One Home**, the repository must be
organized into four logical zones:

### Zone 1: Orchestration & Factory Core (Layers 0, 1, 3)

* `AGENTS.md` (canonical Layer 0)
* `CONTEXT.md` (canonical Layer 1 router)
* `_config/` (invariants, conventions, hardware limits, glossary)
* `_templates/` (contract and checkpoint templates)
* `_tools/` (deterministic validators and test scripts)
* `shared/` (mathematical, vision, netcode, and shader schemas)

### Zone 2: Sequential Execution Stages (Layers 2, 4)

* `stages/`
* `01-probe/`: Reconnaissance and environment inspection.
* `02-controller/`: Math, OpenCV calibration, and controller logic.
* `03-execute/`: Autonomous execution, CDP actions, and performance tracking.



### Zone 3: Benchmarks & Historical Verification (Layer 4)

* `benchmarks/`
* `gauntlet_a_voxel_fps/`: 20 Hz multiplayer FPS gauntlet.
* `gauntlet_b_splatbox/`: OpenCV centroid tracking gauntlet.
* `gauntlet_c_aquarium/`: Three.js shader aquarium gauntlet.


* `phases/`: Sealed verification registry (Phases 1 through 58).
* `phases/status_ledger.json`: Master receipt database.

### Zone 4: Research, Memory, & Substrate Layer

* `references/`: Upstream manuals and theoretical papers.
* `skills/`: Agent skills directory (`icm-architect`, `icm-stage-compiler`).
* `.config/`: Systemd services (`hermes-llama-server.service`,
`hermes-openviking.service`).
* `.local/bin/`: Daemon binaries and launch scripts.
* `.hermes/`: Substrate configuration (`config.yaml`, rules, memories, and
skills).

---

## 6. Non-Breaking Migration & Cleanup Safety Architecture

Reorganizing a production repository requires strict safety boundaries.
We establish these non-negotiable rules to ensure zero regressions:

### 6.1 The Immutable Keep-Out Inventory (Do Not Delete or Move)

These components are essential for system operation and must remain in their
established locations:

| System Component | Established Path | Invariant Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Inference Daemon Service** |
`.config/systemd/user/hermes-llama-server.service` | Bound to systemd user
manager. Required for local Port 8138 inference. |
| **Memory Daemon Service** | `.config/systemd/user/hermes-openviking.service` |
Bound to systemd user manager. Required for local Port 1933 memory. |
| **Inference Daemon Binary** | `.local/bin/llama-server` | Configured with
exact CPU affinity (cores 0-5), 128K context, and MTP drafter. |
| **Agent Configuration** | `.hermes/config.yaml` | Canonical upstream
configuration version 46. |
| **Upstream Submodule** | `.hermes/hermes-agent` | Enforces Rule 9 (Substrate
Purity). Must contain 0 modified files. |
| **Layer 0 Map** | `AGENTS.md` | Primary Layer 0 operating protocol (< 60
lines). |
| **Layer 1 Router** | `CONTEXT.md` | Root task router (< 80 lines). |
| **Verification Ledger** | `phases/status_ledger.json` | Master cryptographic
receipt database for Phases 1 to 58. |
| **Phase Verification Gates** | `phases/phase_*/03_verify/contract.sh` |
Deterministic verification contracts for all 58 sealed phases. |
| **Active Gauntlets** | `benchmarks/gauntlet_a_voxel_fps/`<br>

<br>`benchmarks/gauntlet_b_splatbox/`<br>

<br>`benchmarks/gauntlet_c_aquarium/` | Certified testbeds for netcode, computer
vision, and WebGL shaders. |

### 6.2 The Four-Category Triage Framework

Every file in the repository must be assigned to one of four triage categories:

1. **Category A: Retain In Place (Critical Substrate Core)**
* Components from the Keep-Out Inventory.
* Core root configuration: `Makefile`, `.gitignore`, `pyproject.toml`,
`package.json`.


2. **Category B: Consolidate to ICM Factory (Layer 3 Architecture)**
* Scattered documentation moved into `_config/` or `shared/`.
* Ad-hoc scripts moved into `_tools/`.
* Example: Move domain invariants to `_config/invariants.md`.


3. **Category C: Archive to Historical Vault (Zero Context Clutter)**
* Completed evaluation dumps, old test artifacts, and legacy benchmark reports.
* Relocated into `archive/` or retained inside corresponding `phases/`.
* Leaves the workspace root clean and readable.


4. **Category D: Prune / Safe Deletion**
* Stale build caches, empty temporary directories, and duplicate backups.
* Pruning occurs only after confirming zero references in contracts or scripts.



### 6.3 Phased Verification Protocol

Every relocation must follow a five-step execution cycle:

1. **Pre-Flight Check**: Verify that `python3 _tools/hermes_icm_validator.py`
and `git status` pass.
2. **Atomic Git Move**: Use `git mv <source> <destination>` to preserve commit
history.
3. **Compatibility Bridge**: When scripts expect a legacy path, deploy a
relative symlink.
4. **Post-Move Gate Verification**: Execute `bash
phases/phase_58_.../03_verify/contract.sh` to confirm zero broken dependencies.
5. **Rollback Assurance**: Every relocation must have a defined reverse command
(`git mv <destination> <source>`).

    try:
        return zf.read(prefix + rel_path).decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
    except Exception as e:
        return f"ERROR: {e}"

# Layer 3: Hermes System-Wide Invariants (Tripartite 60% Core)

## 1. Hardware and VRAM Envelope Invariants

* Host OS: Fedora Kinoite 44 (x86_64, rpm-ostree immutable atomic desktop).
* Discrete GPU: NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3050 Laptop GPU (6,144 MiB physical GDDR6).
* Hard VRAM Safety Ceiling (Active Inference): 5,800.0 MiB maximum under
131,072-token (128K) context with Q4_0 TurboQuant KV cache (-ctk q4_0 -ctv
q4_0).
* Quiescent VRAM Ceiling (Daemon Stopped): <= 100.0 MiB (40 MiB nominal idle at
~55°C, 12W) when hermes-llama-server.service is stopped.
* Display Compositor Offload: Wayland compositor must run on the Intel
integrated GPU (Disp.A: Off on NVIDIA nvidia-smi).
* CPU Vision Offload: Computer vision centroid calculations (OpenCV
HoughCircles) must run on host CPU (0 MiB GPU VRAM, < 5 ms frame latency).

## 2. Canonical Network Port Map

* Port 8138: Local llama-server OpenAI-compatible inference engine
(http://127.0.0.1:8138/v1) and /slots endpoint.
* Port 8642: Hermes Agent Gateway API server (http://127.0.0.1:8642/v1).
* Port 1933: OpenViking persistent memory graph daemon (http://127.0.0.1:1933).
* Port 8139: Dedicated SmolVLM2 vision daemon (on-demand).
* Port 9222: Chromium / Chrome DevTools Protocol (CDP) debugging endpoint
(http://127.0.0.1:9222).

## 3. Execution and Verification Invariants

* Champion Primary Substrate: xbill9/gemma-4-E4B-it-qat-q4_0-exact.gguf (4,169
MiB VRAM at 128K).
* Secondary Substrate: unsloth/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-E4B-UD-Q4_K_XL.gguf
(4,975 MiB VRAM at 128K).
* Deprecated Substrate: google/gemma-4-E4B-it-qat-q4_0-gguf (prohibited for tool
calling due to QAT logit shift).
* Read-Before-Edit Law: Every fresh-context stage must invoke read_file on
existing disk targets prior to calling file-edit tools.
* Headless Runtime Gate: Every browser/WebGL deliverable must pass the 4-Gate
Playwright Runtime Audit (0 console errors, 0 page errors, 0 HTTP 4xx/5xx
responses, 1280x720 non-degenerate pixel variance).

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Automated 8-Point ICM Walk Test and Workspace Contract Validator."""
import json
import os
import re
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

def count_lines(path: str) -> int:
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return len(f.readlines())

def validate_workspace() -> int:
    errors = []
    agents_md = os.path.join(ROOT, "AGENTS.md")
    context_md = os.path.join(ROOT, "CONTEXT.md")
    invariants_md = os.path.join(ROOT, "_config", "invariants.md")
    for req in [agents_md, context_md, invariants_md]:
        if not os.path.isfile(req):
            errors.append(f"Missing core file: {req}")
    if os.path.isfile(agents_md) and count_lines(agents_md) > 60:
        errors.append("Layer 0 AGENTS.md exceeds 60-line limit")
    if os.path.isfile(context_md) and count_lines(context_md) > 80:
        errors.append("Layer 1 CONTEXT.md exceeds 80-line limit")
    for ptr in ["CLAUDE.md", "IDENTITY.md"]:
        p = os.path.join(ROOT, ptr)
        if os.path.isfile(p) and count_lines(p) > 5:
            errors.append(f"{ptr} must be a 1-line pointer to AGENTS.md (One
Fact, One Home)")
    stage_dirs = ["stages/01-probe", "stages/02-controller",
"stages/03-execute",
                  "benchmarks/gauntlet_a_voxel_fps",
"benchmarks/gauntlet_b_splatbox",
                  "benchmarks/gauntlet_c_aquarium"]
    for sd in stage_dirs:
        cpath = os.path.join(ROOT, sd, "CONTEXT.md")
        if not os.path.isfile(cpath):
            errors.append(f"Missing stage contract: {sd}/CONTEXT.md")
            continue
        if count_lines(cpath) > 80:
            errors.append(f"{sd}/CONTEXT.md exceeds 80-line limit")
        with open(cpath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            txt = f.read()
        for sec in ["## Inputs", "## Process", "## Outputs", "## Check"]:
            if sec not in txt:
                errors.append(f"{sd}/CONTEXT.md missing required section:
{sec}")
        for rel in re.findall(r"`(\.\./[^`]+)`", txt):
            target = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(ROOT, sd, rel))
            if not os.path.exists(target):
                errors.append(f"Broken relative link in {sd}/CONTEXT.md: {rel}")
    report = {"walk_test_passed": len(errors) == 0, "error_count": len(errors),
"errors": errors}
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    return 0 if len(errors) == 0 else 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(validate_workspace())

PATH: _tools/hermes_stage_runner.py
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Autonomous ICM Stage Compiler with llama-server KV Slot Isolation."""
import argparse
import datetime
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import urllib.request

def erase_kv_slot(base_url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:8138") -> bool:
    req = urllib.request.Request(f"{base_url}/slots/0?action=erase",
method="POST",
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
data=b"{}")
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3) as resp:
            return resp.status == 200
    except Exception:
        return False

def run_stage(stage_dir: str, gate_mode: str, skip_llm: bool) -> int:
    stage_name = os.path.basename(stage_dir.rstrip("/"))
    out_dir = os.path.join(stage_dir, "output")
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    kv_erased = erase_kv_slot()
    if not skip_llm:
        prompt_path = os.path.join(out_dir, ".compiler_prompt.txt")
        prompt = (f"Read AGENTS.md and {stage_dir}/CONTEXT.md. Load only the
exact paths in Inputs. "
                  f"Call read_file before editing any existing file. Execute
Process steps, write "
                  f"deliverables to {out_dir}/, and stop immediately.")
        with open(prompt_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(prompt)
        env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k != "HERMES_TUI_RESUME"}
        subprocess.run(["hermes", "chat", "--query-file", prompt_path], env=env,
check=True)
        erase_kv_slot()
    contract_sh = os.path.join(stage_dir, "contract.sh")
    rc = subprocess.run(["bash", contract_sh], check=False).returncode if
os.path.exists(contract_sh) else 0
    now =
datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    verdict = "PASS" if rc == 0 else "FAIL"
    with open(os.path.join(out_dir, "check.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(f"---\nstage_id: \"{stage_name}\"\ntimestamp_utc: \"{now}\"\n"
                f"kv_slot_erased: {str(kv_erased).lower()}\ncontract_exit_code:
{rc}\n"
                f"auditor_verdict: \"{verdict}\"\n---\n# Audit: {verdict}\n")
    decision = "AUTO_APPROVED_BY_CONTRACT" if (gate_mode == "autonomous" and rc
== 0) else (
               "APPROVED" if rc == 0 else "REJECTED")
    with open(os.path.join(out_dir, "checkpoint.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as
f:
        f.write(f"---\nstage_id: \"{stage_name}\"\nhuman_decision:
\"{decision}\"\n"
                f"timestamp_utc: \"{now}\"\n---\n# Checkpoint: {decision}\n")
    print(json.dumps({"stage": stage_name, "verdict": verdict, "checkpoint":
decision, "kv_erased": kv_erased}))
    return rc

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--stage-dir", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--gate-mode", choices=["autonomous", "interactive"],
default="autonomous")
    ap.add_argument("--verify-only", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    sys.exit(run_stage(args.stage_dir, args.gate_mode, args.verify_only))

================================================================================
PATH: _tools/check_vram_limit.py
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Deterministic NVIDIA RTX 3050 VRAM Ceiling & Quiescence Verifier."""
import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys

def check_vram(mode: str, max_active_mib: float, max_quiescent_mib: float) ->
int:
    cmd = ["nvidia-smi",
"--query-gpu=memory.used,memory.total,temperature.gpu,power.draw",
           "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"]
    out = subprocess.check_output(cmd, text=True).strip().splitlines()[0]
    parts = [p.strip() for p in out.split(",")]
    used_mib = float(parts[0])
    total_mib = float(parts[1])
    temp_c = float(parts[2])
    power_w = float(parts[3]) if parts[3] != "[N/A]" else 0.0
    ceiling = max_quiescent_mib if mode == "quiescent" else max_active_mib
    passed = used_mib <= ceiling
    receipt = {
        "mode": mode,
        "vram_used_mib": used_mib,
        "vram_total_mib": total_mib,
        "ceiling_mib": ceiling,
        "headroom_mib": round(ceiling - used_mib, 1),
        "temperature_c": temp_c,
        "power_w": power_w,
        "passed": passed
    }
    print(json.dumps(receipt))
    return 0 if passed else 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--mode", choices=["active", "quiescent"],
default="quiescent")
    ap.add_argument("--max-active", type=float, default=5800.0)
    ap.add_argument("--max-quiescent", type=float, default=100.0)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    sys.exit(check_vram(args.mode, args.max_active, args.max_quiescent))

================================================================================
PATH: _tools/playwright_runtime_audit.py
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Immutable 4-Gate Headless Browser Runtime & Framebuffer Verifier."""
import argparse
import json
import sys
import numpy as np
import cv2
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

def run_audit(url: str, width: int, height: int, min_std: float, out_json: str)
-> int:
    console_errors, page_errors, bad_http, warnings = [], [], [], []
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        browser = p.chromium.launch(headless=True)
        page = browser.new_page(viewport={"width": width, "height": height})
        page.on("console", lambda m: console_errors.append(m.text) if m.type ==
"error" else (
            warnings.append(m.text) if m.type == "warning" else None))
        page.on("pageerror", lambda exc: page_errors.append(str(exc)))
        page.on("response", lambda r: bad_http.append(f"{r.status}:{r.url}") if
r.status >= 400 else None)
        page.goto(url, wait_until="networkidle", timeout=15000)
        page.wait_for_timeout(1500)
        canvas = page.locator("canvas").first
        box = canvas.bounding_box() if canvas.count() > 0 else None
        png_bytes = canvas.screenshot() if box else b""
        browser.close()
    img = cv2.imdecode(np.frombuffer(png_bytes, np.uint8), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR) if
png_bytes else None
    pixel_std = float(np.std(img)) if img is not None else 0.0
    geom_ok = bool(box and int(round(box["width"])) == width and
int(round(box["height"])) == height)
    passed = (len(console_errors) == 0 and len(page_errors) == 0 and
len(bad_http) == 0
              and len(warnings) == 0 and geom_ok and pixel_std >= min_std)
    receipt = {"url": url, "passed": passed, "console_errors":
len(console_errors),
               "page_errors": len(page_errors), "warnings": len(warnings),
               "http_4xx_5xx": len(bad_http), "canvas_box": box, "pixel_std":
round(pixel_std, 2)}
    if out_json:
        with open(out_json, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(receipt, f, indent=2)
    print(json.dumps(receipt))
    return 0 if passed else 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--url", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--width", type=int, default=1280)
    ap.add_argument("--height", type=int, default=720)
    ap.add_argument("--min-std", type=float, default=5.0)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    sys.exit(run_audit(args.url, args.width, args.height, args.min_std,
args.out))

================================================================================
PATH: scripts/hermes_icm_validator.py
================================================================================
import os
import pathlib
import re
from typing import List, Dict

WORKSPACE_ROOT =
pathlib.Path("/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes")
ICM_LAYERS = {
    0: "AGENTS.md",
    1: "CONTEXT.md",
    2: "stages/01-spec/CONTEXT.md",
    3: "rules/",
    4: "artifacts/"
}
LINE_CAPS = {
    "CONTEXT.md": 80,
    "rules/": 200
}

def check_file_existence(path: pathlib.Path) -> bool:
    """Checks if a file or directory exists."""
    return path.exists()

def count_lines(path: pathlib.Path) -> int:
    """Counts lines in a file."""
    try:
        with open(path, 'r') as f:
            return sum(1 for line in f)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return -1

def check_4_column_table(path: pathlib.Path) -> bool:
    """Checks if a CONTEXT.md file contains the 4-column table standard."""
    try:
        content = path.read_text()
        # Simple regex check for a markdown table structure with 4 columns
        # Requires at least a header row and a separator row
        table_pattern = r"\|.*?\|.*?\|.*?\|.*?\|.*?\|"
        return bool(re.search(table_pattern, content))
    except Exception:
        return False

def run_8_point_walk_test() -> Dict[str, bool]:
    """Executes the 8-Point Walk Test based on ICM invariants."""
    results = {}
    print("--- ICM 8-Point Walk Test ---")

    # 1. Check Layer Existence (0-4)
    for layer, file_or_dir in ICM_LAYERS.items():
        path = WORKSPACE_ROOT / file_or_dir
        exists = check_file_existence(path)
        results[f"Layer {layer} Exists"] = exists
        print(f"Layer {layer} ({file_or_dir}): {'PASS' if exists else 'FAIL'}")

    # 2. Check AGENTS.md existence (Layer 0)
    results["L0_AGENTS_md_present"] = check_file_existence(WORKSPACE_ROOT /
"AGENTS.md")
    print(f"L0 AGENTS.md: {'PASS' if results['L0_AGENTS_md_present'] else
'FAIL'}")

    # 3. Check CONTEXT.md line cap (Layer 1)
    context_path = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "CONTEXT.md"
    lines = count_lines(context_path)
    cap = LINE_CAPS.get("CONTEXT.md", 0)
    results["L1_CONTEXT_md_cap"] = lines <= cap
    print(f"L1 CONTEXT.md lines ({lines}): {'PASS' if
results['L1_CONTEXT_md_cap'] else 'FAIL'} (Cap: {cap})")

    # 4. Check Stage Context line cap (Layer 2)
    stage_context_path = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "stages/01-spec/CONTEXT.md"
    lines = count_lines(stage_context_path)
    cap = LINE_CAPS.get("CONTEXT.md", 0)
    results["L2_STAGE_CONTEXT_md_cap"] = lines <= cap
    print(f"L2 Stage CONTEXT.md lines ({lines}): {'PASS' if
results['L2_STAGE_CONTEXT_md_cap'] else 'FAIL'} (Cap: {cap})")

    # 5. Check Rules/Config line cap (Layer 3)
    rules_path = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "rules/"
    if rules_path.is_dir():
        for item in rules_path.iterdir():
            if item.is_file():
                lines = count_lines(item)
                cap = LINE_CAPS.get("rules/", 0)
                results[f"L3_Rule_Cap_{item.name}"] = lines <= cap
                print(f"L3 Rule {item.name} lines ({lines}): {'PASS' if
results[f'L3_Rule_Cap_{item.name}'] else 'FAIL'} (Cap: {cap})")
    else:
        results["L3_Rules_Dir_Exists"] = False
        print("L3 Rules Dir: FAIL (Directory not found)")

    # 6. Check 4-Column Table Standard (Layer 1)
    results["L1_4_Column_Table"] = check_4_column_table(context_path)
    print(f"L1 4-Column Table: {'PASS' if results['L1_4_Column_Table'] else
'FAIL'}")

    # 7. Check Layer 4 Artifacts existence
    results["L4_Artifacts_Dir_Exists"] = check_file_existence(WORKSPACE_ROOT /
"artifacts/")
    print(f"L4 Artifacts Dir: {'PASS' if results['L4_Artifacts_Dir_Exists'] else
'FAIL'}")

    # 8. Overall Status
    all_passed = all(results.values())
    print("\\n--- SUMMARY ---")
    print(f"ICM Validation Status: {'SUCCESS' if all_passed else 'FAILURE'}")
    return results

if __name__ == "__main__":
    validation_results = run_8_point_walk_test()
    # Exit with non-zero code on failure for CI/automation
    if not all(validation_results.values()):
        exit(1)

#!/usr/bin/env bash

S3="${BENCH_DIR}/stages/03-execute/output/telemetry.json"

echo "=== [Gauntlet B: Splatbox 3-Stage ICM Contract Verification] ==="

# 1. Verify Hardware VRAM Invariant (<= 5800 MiB)
VRAM_USED=$(nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits |
head -n 1 | tr -d ' ')
if [ "${VRAM_USED}" -gt 5800 ]; then
  echo "[FAIL] Hardware VRAM ceiling breached: ${VRAM_USED} MiB > 5800 MiB"
  exit 1
fi
echo "[PASS] Hardware VRAM within invariant: ${VRAM_USED} MiB <= 5800 MiB"

# 2. Verify Stage 1 Gate
jq -e '.pointer_lock_active == true' "${S1}" > /dev/null
echo "[PASS] Stage 01-probe certified: PointerLock active."

# 3. Verify Stage 2 Gate
jq -e '.targets_found_ge_1 == true' "${S2}" > /dev/null
echo "[PASS] Stage 02-controller certified: Closed-loop target centroid
verified."

# 4. Verify Stage 3 Match Telemetry (Zero Fake Green)
jq -e '.score > 0 and .duration_sec >= 55 and .frames_processed >= 120 and
.embeddinggemma2_verified == true' "${S3}" > /dev/null
SCORE=$(jq -r '.score' "${S3}")
FRAMES=$(jq -r '.frames_processed' "${S3}")
echo "[PASS] Stage 03-execute certified: Score=${SCORE}, Frames=${FRAMES}."
echo "=== [CERTIFIED: GAUNTLET B 100% PASS] ==="

# Hermes Production Benchmark Session Registry

> This file incrementally records every completed benchmark task session.
> Each entry contains the task metadata, evaluation outcome, and exact commands
to review Hermes's full internal thinking, tool invocations, and responses in
read-only mode.

---

## Quick Reference Commands

* **Interactive Session Review (CLI / TUI)**:
  ```bash
  hermes --resume <SESSION_ID>

```

* **Export Full Trace to Clean Markdown**:
```bash
hermes sessions export --session-id <SESSION_ID> --format md ./session_exports/

```


* **Export Interactive HTML Trace**:
```bash
hermes sessions export --session-id <SESSION_ID> --format html
./session_exports/<SESSION_ID>.html

```

def _collect_domain_c_git_diff(testbed_root: Path) -> tuple[list[str], str]:
    """Extracts modified scenario .py files and SHA-256 diff hash in Git
testbeds."""
    def _filter_py(lines: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        return [
            f.strip() for f in lines
            if f.strip().endswith(".py")
            and not os.path.basename(f.strip()).startswith(("test_",
"benchmark_"))
            and os.path.basename(f.strip()) != "conftest.py"
        ]

    try:
        raw = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "-C", str(testbed_root), "diff", "--name-only", "HEAD"],
            text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        ).splitlines()
        mod_files = _filter_py(raw)
        diff_ref = "HEAD"

        if not mod_files:
            raw_prev = subprocess.check_output(
                ["git", "-C", str(testbed_root), "diff", "--name-only",
"HEAD~1", "HEAD"],
                text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            ).splitli

def calculate_fibonacci(n):
    """Calculates the nth Fibonacci number."""
    if n <= 0:
        return 0
    elif n == 1:
        return 1
    else:
        a, b = 0, 1
        for _ in range(2, n + 1):
            a, b = b, a + b
        return b

if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("Fibonacci sequence:")
    for i in range(10):
        print(f"F({i}): {calculate_fibonacci(i)}")

# Layer 3 Reference: Anti-Rationalization Tables (Addy Osmani Heuristics)

> **Invariance Rule**: This reference refutes common LLM rationalisations before
execution.
> **Line Limit**: Strictly capped <= 200 lines.

## 1. Multi-Turn Dialogue & Tool Rationalisations

| Common Rationalisation / Excuse | Reality & Structural Barrier | Mandatory
Deterministic Action |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| *"The customer didn't give their email, so I cannot lookup the order."* | The
store database provides `find_user_id_by_name_zip(first, last, zip)` and order
ID queries. | **Never bail out**. Immediately invoke `find_user_id_by_name_zip`
using customer name and zip code. |
| *"The database returned an error, so I must transfer to a human agent."* |
Database errors are frequently caused by typos in customer input (e.g. invalid
zip or swapped letters). | **Ask for clarification**. Verify parameters with
customer or query alternative search indices. |
| *"I should ask the customer which store or website URL they bought from."* |
You are the dedicated internal agent for *this* store. Asking this breaks
persona. | **Never ask retailer URL**. Ground all inquiries in internal
product/order records. |
| *"I assume the pending order allows payment method mutation without
checking."* | Order modification requires checking policy constraints (e.g.
pending vs shipped status). | **Always call `get_order_details`** first to
inspect order status before mutating state. |

exception tracebacks and next causal step.

active FastMCP tool schema.

immediately without proceeding.

documentation files.

# Layer 3 Factory Rule: Hardware & 128K VRAM Invariants
(hardware_vram_invariants.md)

> Layer 3 Reference Contract: Physical Hardware Boundaries & Verification Gates.
> Rule: File must remain strictly under 200 lines.

---

## 1. Dedicated Physical GPU Envelope

* **GPU**: NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3050 Laptop GPU (6,144 MiB physical GDDR6).
* **Display Isolation (Disp.A: Off)**: Wayland compositor (`kwin_wayland`) runs
on the integrated AMD Radeon GPU, using only 6 MiB on the NVIDIA card. Full
6,138 MiB is dedicated to compute.
* **Hard NVML Ceiling**: Active GPU VRAM must never exceed **5,800.0 MiB**
(`nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits`),
guaranteeing >= 344 MiB physical safety margin.

---

## 2. Canonical 128K Engine & KV Cache Invariant (Port 8138)

* **Context Window**: `-c 131072` (`context_length: 131072`), satisfying
upstream `MINIMUM_CONTEXT_LENGTH = 64000` (`agent/model_metadata.py`) and
optimal `ContextCompressor` thresholds.
* **KV Cache Quantization**: Google TurboQuant 4-bit KV cache (`-ctk q4_0 -ctv
q4_0 -fa 1`), keeping the 128K KV buffer at ~1,075 MiB.
* **CPU & Swap Isolation**: Port 8138 inference pinned to cores 0–5 (`taskset -c
0-5 -t 6 -tb 6`); cores 6–11 reserved for host OS, Port 8139 CPU embeddings, and
Port 1933 OpenViking; backed by 15.3 GiB zram + 2,048 MiB btrfs NOCOW swapfile
(`/var/swap/swapfile`).

---

# Root Operational Invariants (Layer 2)

> Architectural Standard: Jake Van Clief's ICM (arXiv:2603.16021)
> Strict Line Budget: <= 80 lines

1. **Hardware VRAM Hard Ceiling**:
   - Active GPU memory on discrete NVIDIA RTX 3050 Laptop GPU (6,144 MiB
physical GDDR6) must NEVER exceed 5,800.0 MiB under any workload.
   - Enforce via continuous NVML polling (`nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used
--format=csv,noheader,nounits`).

2. **Upstream Substrate Purity**:
   - `.hermes/hermes-agent` submodule must remain 100% pure and untouched (`git
status --porcelain` returns 0 lines).
   - Zero monkey-patches, monkey-patching decorators, or vendor overrides.

3. **Zero Bespoke Wrappers Invariant**:
   - Never author bespoke Python wrappers or custom plugins in `scripts/` or
`plugins/`.
   - All customizations must be achieved via declarative `config.yaml` or
portable skills in `~/.hermes/skills/`.

4. **Working Context Window Discipline**:
   - Maintain active conversation working context strictly between 2,000 and
8,000 tokens per stage.
   - Never run unbounded monolithic multi-turn sessions that exceed 12 turns.

# Root Operational Rules (Layer 2)

> Architectural Standard: Jake Van Clief's ICM (arXiv:2603.16021)
> Strict Line Budget: <= 120 lines

1. **Deterministic Sequential Stage Execution**:
   - Every complex engineering requirement must traverse the 4-stage pipeline
sequentially:
     `01-spec` -> `02-plan` -> `03-execute` -> `04-verify`.
   - Never skip directly to execution without an approved specification and
signed contract.

2. **Contract-First Verification**:
   - Each operational directory must contain a deterministic `contract.sh` with
`set -euo pipefail`.
   - All tests must run headlessly and verify syntax, AST structure, and exit
code 0.

3. **Two-Strike Tabu Rule**:
   - If an agent generates code that fails verification twice, it must revert to
the last green checkpoint and try an alternative architecture.

4. **Normalized Output Rule**:
   - Autonomous verification evaluations must emit exact ground-truth values
without conversational conversational prefixes.

5. **Tool Call Protocol**:
   - All tool calls must be emitted in structured JSON format; plain text leaks
are strictly forbidden.

# Root Epistemic References Index (Layer 3)

> Architectural Standard: Jake Van Clief's ICM (arXiv:2603.16021)
> Strict Line Budget: <= 200 lines

## Core Methodologies & Literature
1. **Interpretable Context Methodology (ICM / MWP)**:
   - Reference: Jake Van Clief & David McDermott (arXiv:2603.16021)
   - Principles: File-system state machines, 60/30/10 layering, working context
between 2k and 8k tokens.

2. **Nested Learning**:
   - Reference: Behrouz et al., Google Research (arXiv:2512.24695)
   - Principles: Multi-timescale associative memory: Level 1 (KV/SSM), Level 2
(Stage surprise score), Level 3 (Ebbinghaus vault).

3. **Dream-RSI (Autonomous Recursive Self-Improvement)**:
   - Reference: Zheng et al. (arXiv:2609.14858)
   - Principles: Dual-pass bracketed schedule, off-policy Doubly Robust
estimation, evolving world discovery trees.

4. **Agentic Design Patterns**:
   - Reference: Antonio Gullí (21 Patterns)
   - Principles: Anti-reflection loops, anti-memory hoarding, tool-grounding
gates, deterministic contracts.

# Shared Reference: Multiplayer Netcode Protocol (Gauntlet A)

## Protocol Invariants
- Transport: WebSocket on Port 8080.
- Broadcast Frequency: 20 Hz fixed tick loop (50 ms interval).
- Client Packet Schema: Supports `data.type || data.t`.
- Handshake Sequence: Server sends `join` / `WELCOME` on connection with client
ID.
- Position Broadcast: Serialized x, y, z positions with interpolation.
- Voxel Splat Broadcast: Position, color hex, and voxel mutation type.

# Shared Reference: Shader & Buoyancy Conventions (Gauntlet C)

## Three.js Shader Invariants
- Library Version: Three.js `three@0.165.0`.
- ShaderMaterial Uniforms Property: Strictly plural `uniforms: { ... }`.
Singular `uniform:` is invalid.
- Water GPGPU: Dual framebuffer wave PDE ping-pong on floating-point textures.
- Caustics Shader: Differential area mapping using dFdx/dFdy.
- Buoyancy Physics: Clamped spherical-cap volume integration with drag damping.

# Shared Reference: Vision and Centroid Schemas (Gauntlet B)

## Vision Invariants
- Target Canvas: `#gameCanvas` at 1280x720 CSS pixels.
- High-DPI Scale Ratio: `canvas.width / rect.width` and `canvas.height /
rect.height`.
- Aiming Algorithm: OpenCV CPU `cv2.HoughCircles` detection (dp=1.2, minDist=30,
param1=50, param2=30).
- Latency Constraint: < 5 ms frame latency on CPU (0 MiB GPU VRAM).
- Precision Constraint: < 5 px aiming error relative to target center.
- Telemetry Schema: JSON with `frames_captured`, `score`, `elapsed_s`, and
`vram_mib`.

# 01-probe - Browser and Canvas State Discovery

One job: Probe the browser canvas and write target metadata to
output/probe_state.json.

## Inputs

| Source | File/Location | Section/Scope | Why |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| System Invariants | `../../_config/invariants.md` | Network Ports | Port 9222
connection |

import logging
import uuid
from datetime import datetime

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__).setLevel(logging.INFO)

def process_telemetry_record(record: dict) -> dict | None:
    """
    Processes a single telemetry record, handling missing fields gracefully.
    Returns the processed record or None if input is invalid.
    """
    # 3. Input validation: Ensure the record is a dictionary
    if not isinstance(record, dict):
        logger.error(f"Received non-dictionary record type: {type(record)}")
        return None

    # 1. Safely handle event_id
    event_id = record.get('event_id')
    if event_id is None:
        fallback_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        logger.warning(f"Missing 'event_id' in record. Using fallback UUID:
{fallback_id}")
        event_id = fallback_id

    # 2. Validate timestamp and add default if missing
    timestamp = record.get('ts') or record.get('timestamp',
datetime.utcnow().isoformat())

    payload = record.get('payload', {})

    return {
        'processed_id': f"EVT_{event_id}",
        'timestamp': timestamp,
        'metrics': payload.get('metrics', {})
    }

# Example usage (for testing purposes)
if __name__ == '__main__':
    test_record = {'event_id': '12345', 'timestamp': '2023-10-01T10:00:00Z',
'data': {'metrics': [1, 2]}}
    print(process_telemetry_record(test_record))

    legacy_record = {'id': 'LEGACY_99', 'payload': {'metrics': [3, 4]}}
    print(process_telemetry-record(legacy_record))

    invalid_record = "not a d
...[TRUNCATED]...

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Verification Contract for Phase 46: Zero-VRAM Disk-KV MCTS Engine
set -euo pipefail

WORKSPACE="/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes"
RECEIPT="/tmp/hermes_verified_receipt.json"

echo "=== [Phase 46 Contract: Zero-VRAM Disk-KV MCTS Engine] ==="

# 1. Execute Ruff Linter on MCTS Module
python3 -m ruff check "$WORKSPACE/scripts/hermes_disk_kv_mcts.py"

# 2. Comprehensive Test Suite
python3 -c '
from scripts.hermes_disk_kv_mcts import DiskKVMCTSEngine,
compute_deterministic_value

print("[1/2] Testing Deterministic Value Function V_det...")
# Case 1: Perfect pass (AST valid, 0 ruff errors, 100% tests, Gate2 pass)
v_max = compute_deterministic_value(True, 0, 1.0, True)
assert v_max == 1.0, f"Expected 1.0, got {v_max}"

# Case 2: Partial pass (AST valid, 1 ruff error, 50% tests, Gate2 pass)
v_mid = compute_deterministic_value(True, 1, 0.5, True)
assert 0.65 <= v_mid <= 0.75, f"Unexpected mid score: {v_mid}"

# Case 3: Syntax error (AST invalid, 0% tests)
v_fail = compute_deterministic_value(False, 2, 0.0, True)
assert v_fail <= 0.30, f"Unexpected fail score: {v_fail}"

print("[2/2] Testing Disk-KV Time-Multiplexed MCTS Search...")
engine = DiskKVMCTSEngine(kv_dir="/tmp/hermes_mcts_test_kv")

prompt_stub = "def multiply_list(numbers: list[int]) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Multiply
elements\"\"\""

def generate_actions(code):
    return [
        ("```python\ndef multiply_list(items: list[int]) -> int:\n    res = 1\n
  for x in items:\n        res *= x\n    return res\n```", 0.5), # Correct logic
+ renamed param + fences
        ("def multiply_list(numbers: list[int]) -> int:\n    return 0", 0.3),
                                                        # Incorrect logic
        ("def multiply_list(numbers: list[int]) -> int:\n    return", 0.2),
                                                         # Syntax error
    ]

def evaluate_test(code):
    loc = {}
    exec(code, {}, loc)
    fn = loc["multiply_list"]
    assert fn([2, 3, 4]) == 24
    assert fn([]) == 1
    return 1.0

best_node = engine.run_mcts_search(
    root_code="",
    candidates_generator=generate_actions,
    prompt_stub=prompt_stub,
    test_fn=evaluate_test,
    iterations=3
)

assert best_node.deterministic_value == 1.0, f"Expected 1.0, got
{best_node.deterministic_value}"
assert "for x in numbers:" in best_node.action_code
assert engine.save_count >= 1
assert engine.restore_count >= 1

print("All Disk-KV MCTS verification contracts PASSED.")
'

# 3. Hardware Invariant Checks
VRAM_USED=$(nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits |
head -n 1 | awk '{print $1}')
python3 -c "assert float('$VRAM_USED') <= 5400.0, f'VRAM ceiling breached:
$VRAM_USED MiB'"
test -z "$(git -C "$WORKSPACE/.hermes/hermes-agent" status --porcelain)"

# 4. Cryptographic Sealing of Verified Receipt
python3 -c '
import json
json.dump({
    "exitstatus": 0,
    "workspace": "'"$WORKSPACE"'",
    "active_phase": "phase_46_disk_kv_mcts_engine_swa_full_dev_shm",
    "all_phases_verified": False,
    "nvml_vram_used_mib": float("'"$VRAM_USED"'"),
    "nvml_vram_limit_mib": 5400.0,
    "disk_kv_mcts_active": True,
    "deterministic_v_det_active": True,
    "upstream_git_clean": True
}, open("'"$RECEIPT"'", "w"), indent=2)
'

echo "=== [PHASE 46 VERIFIED: Zero-VRAM Disk-KV MCTS Engine Sealed] ==="

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Verification Contract for Phase 48: AST Canonizer v3 & 768-Token Ephemeral

set -euo pipefail

WORKSPACE="/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes"

echo "=== [Phase 48 Contract: AST Canonizer v3 & 768-Token Tool Budget] ==="

# 1. Execute Ruff Linter on Modified Files
python3 -m ruff check "$WORKSPACE/scripts/hermes_ast_canonizer.py" \
                      "$WORKSPACE/.hermes/mcp_ephemeral_bridge.py"

# 2. Comprehensive Empirical Verification of AST Canonizer v3 & 6 SLM Traps
python3 -c '
import ast
from scripts.hermes_ast_canonizer import (
    CanonicalSignatureTransformer,
    sanitize_and_canonize_edit_surface,
    extract_canonical_function_info
)

print("[1/3] Testing AST Canonizer v3 against 6 SLM Syntax Traps...")

# Trap 1: Unclosed deliberation tag on budget boundary
raw_trap1 = "<deliberation>Analysing algorithm complexity\n```python\ndef
solve(x: int) -> int:\n    return x + 1\n```"
res1 = sanitize_and_canonize_edit_surface(raw_trap1, "def solve(x: int) ->
int:")
assert "Analysing" not in res1, "Trap 1 failed: deliberation not stripped"
assert "def solve(x: int) -> int:" in res1
ast.parse(res1.replace("```python\n", "").replace("\n```", ""))
print("  ✓ Trap 1 (Unclosed deliberation tag): RESOLVED")

# Trap 2: Multi-fence illustrative prefix collision
raw_trap2 = "```python\n# Snippet\ndef helper_foo():
pass\n```\nExplanation...\n```python\ndef solve(x: int) -> int:\n    return x *
2\n```"
res2 = sanitize_and_canonize_edit_surface(raw_trap2, "def solve(x: int) ->
int:")
assert "def solve(x: int) -> int:" in res2
assert "return x * 2" in res2
ast.parse(res2.replace("```python\n", "").replace("\n```", ""))
print("  ✓ Trap 2 (Multi-fence prefix collision): RESOLVED")

# Trap 3: Standalone function missing preamble imports (NameError prevention)
stub_trap3 = "from typing import List, Optional\nimport math\n\ndef
has_close_elements(numbers: List[float], threshold: float) -> bool:\n
\"\"\"Docstring\"\"\""
raw_trap3 = "```python\ndef has_close_elements(numbers: List[float], threshold:
float) -> bool:\n    return True\n```"
res3 = sanitize_and_canonize_edit_surface(raw_trap3, stub_trap3)
assert "from typing import List, Optional" in res3, "Trap 3 failed: preamble
imports dropped"
assert "import math" in res3
ast.parse(res3.replace("```python\n", "").replace("\n```", ""))
print("  ✓ Trap 3 (Missing preamble imports): RESOLVED")

# Trap 4: Helper-only standalone FunctionDef hijack
stub_trap4 = "def target_func(data: list) -> int:\n"
raw_trap4 = "```python\ndef helper_util(a: int) -> int:\n    return a + 1\n```\n
   return helper_util(data[0])"
res4 = sanitize_and_canonize_edit_surface(raw_trap4, stub_trap4)
assert "def target_func(data: list) -> int:" in res4
ast.parse(res4.replace("```python\n", "").replace("\n```", ""))
print("  ✓ Trap 4 (Helper-only FunctionDef hijack): RESOLVED")

# Trap 5: Asymmetrical first-line indentation in body completions
stub_trap5 = "def find_index(items: list) -> int:\n"
raw_trap5 = "for idx, item in enumerate(items):\n        if item > 0:\n
  return idx\n    return -1"
res5 = sanitize_and_canonize_edit_surface(raw_trap5, stub_trap5)
assert "def find_index(items: list) -> int:" in res5
ast.parse(res5.replace("```python\n", "").replace("\n```", ""))
print("  ✓ Trap 5 (Asymmetrical first-line indentation): RESOLVED")

# Trap 6: Unrecovered tail-line truncation on max-tokens limit
stub_trap6 = "def compute_sum(n: int) -> int:\n"
raw_trap6 = "    total = sum(range(n))\n    return total\n    # Incomplete
trailing line or comment +"
res6 = sanitize_and_canonize_edit_surface(raw_trap6, stub_trap6)
assert "return total" in res6
ast.parse(res6.replace("```python\n", "").replace("\n```", ""))
print("  ✓ Trap 6 (Unrecovered tail-line truncation): RESOLVED")

# Phase 44 Canonical Regression Verification
stub_mad = "def mean_absolute_deviation(numbers: list[float]) -> float:\n
\"\"\"Docstring\"\"\""
raw_mad = "```python\ndef mean_absolute_deviation(len_of_list: list[float]) ->
float:\n    total = sum(len_of_list)\n    return total / len(len_of_list)\n```"
canon_mad = sanitize_and_canonize_edit_surface(raw_mad, stub_mad)
assert "numbers" in canon_mad
assert "len_of_list" not in canon_mad

stub_fib = "def fib(n: int) -> int:"
raw_fib = "def fib(self, n: int) -> int:\n    if n <= 1:\n        return n\n
return self.fib(n - 1) + self.fib(n - 2)"
canon_fib = sanitize_and_canonize_edit_surface(raw_fib, stub_fib)
assert "self" not in canon_fib
assert "return fib(n - 1) + fib(n - 2)" in canon_fib
print("  ✓ Phase 44 Regression Invariants: PRESERVED")

print("[2/3] Testing Ephemeral Bridge Configuration Invariants...")
import importlib.util
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("bridge",
"'"$WORKSPACE"'/.hermes/mcp_ephemeral_bridge.py")
bridge = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(bridge)
# Verify directives exist
assert hasattr(bridge, "ROLE_CONDITIONING_DIRECTIVE")
assert hasattr(bridge, "CODE_SYNTHESIS_DIRECTIVE")
assert "< 120 tokens" in bridge.ROLE_CONDITIONING_DIRECTIVE or "< 150 tokens" in
bridge.ROLE_CONDITIONING_DIRECTIVE
print("  ✓ Ephemeral Bridge Directives & Parameters: VERIFIED")
'

# 3. Hardware Invariant Checks
VRAM_USED=$(nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits |

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Verification Contract for Phase 49: EmbeddingGemma 2 Autonomous CPU Engine (0
MiB VRAM)
set -euo pipefail

WORKSPACE="/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes"
RECEIPT="/tmp/hermes_verified_receipt.json"

echo "=== [Phase 49 Contract: EmbeddingGemma 2 Autonomous CPU Engine] ==="

# 1. Execute Ruff Linter on Client
python3 -m ruff check "$WORKSPACE/scripts/hermes_embedding_cpu_client.py"

# 2. Systemd Service Unit Verification
SERVICE_FILE="$HOME/.config/systemd/user/hermes-embedding-cpu.service"
test -f "$SERVICE_FILE"
grep -q "CPUAffinity=6-11" "$SERVICE_FILE"
grep -q "CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=" "$SERVICE_FILE"
grep -q "GGML_CUDA_DISABLE=1" "$SERVICE_FILE"
grep -q -- "--port 8139" "$SERVICE_FILE"
grep -q -- "--n-gpu-layers 0" "$SERVICE_FILE"
echo "  ✓ Systemd CPU affinity & zero-VRAM environment flags: VERIFIED"

# 3. Python Verification Suite (7 Prefixes & Matryoshka Representation Learning)
python3 -c '
import math
from scripts.hermes_embedding_cpu_client import (
    EmbeddingGemmaCPUClient,
    TASK_INSTRUCTION_PREFIXES,
    apply_matryoshka_truncation,
    compute_cosine_similarity
)

client = EmbeddingGemmaCPUClient()

print("[1/2] Testing 7 Task Instruction Prefixes...")
expected_tasks = [
    "Classification", "QuestionAnswering", "CodeRetrieval",
    "SearchQuery", "FactChecking", "SemanticSimilarity", "Clustering"
]
for task in expected_tasks:
    assert task in TASK_INSTRUCTION_PREFIXES, f"Missing task prefix: {task}"
    formatted = client.format_task_input("test query", task)
    assert formatted.startswith(TASK_INSTRUCTION_PREFIXES[task])
print("  ✓ All 7 Canonical Task Instruction Prefixes: VERIFIED")

print("[2/2] Testing Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) Truncation &
Normalization...")
for dim in [128, 256, 512, 768]:
    vec = client.get_embedding("Hermes Agent OS Zero-VRAM Architecture",
task="CodeRetrieval", target_dim=dim)
    assert len(vec) == dim, f"Expected dimension {dim}, got {len(vec)}"
    norm = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in vec))
    assert abs(norm - 1.0) < 1e-4, f"L2 normalization failed for dim {dim}:
norm={norm}"

# Test Cosine Similarity logic
v_code = client.get_embedding("def calculate_sum(a, b): return a + b",
task="CodeRetrieval", target_dim=256)
v_diff = client.get_embedding("The geopolitical climate in 1914",
task="SearchQuery", target_dim=256)
sim_same = compute_cosine_similarity(v_code, v_code)
sim_diff = compute_cosine_similarity(v_code, v_diff)
assert abs(sim_same - 1.0) < 1e-4, f"Self-similarity must be 1.0, got
{sim_same}"
assert sim_diff < 0.95, f"Unrelated embeddings too similar: {sim_diff}"
print("  ✓ MRL 128d/256d/512d/768d Scaling & Cosine Similarity: VERIFIED")
'

# 4. Hardware Invariant Checks
VRAM_USED=$(nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits |
head -n 1 | awk '{print $1}')
python3 -c "assert float('$VRAM_USED') <= 5400.0, f'VRAM ceiling breached:
$VRAM_USED MiB'"
test -z "$(git -C "$WORKSPACE/../../Nadia/.hermes/hermes-agent" status

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Verification Contract for Phase 51: IBM STAIR Prefix-Trie Retriever &
Pre-Execution Grounding Gate
set -euo pipefail

WORKSPACE="/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes"
RECEIPT="/tmp/hermes_verified_receipt.json"

echo "=== [Phase 51 Contract: IBM STAIR Prefix-Trie Retriever & Grounding Gate]
==="

# 1. Execute Ruff Linter on Implementation Scripts
python3 -m ruff check "$WORKSPACE/scripts/hermes_stair_retriever.py"
"$WORKSPACE/.hermes/mcp_ephemeral_bridge.py"

# 2. Execute Deterministic STAIR Unit & Integration Test Suite
python3 -c '
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Add workspace to path
sys.path.insert(0, "'"$WORKSPACE"'")

from scripts.hermes_stair_retriever import STAIRLeafNode, STAIRPrefixTrie,
HermesSTAIRVerifier

# Test A: STAIRPrefixTrie Structural Prefix & Exact Resolution
trie = STAIRPrefixTrie()
node1 = STAIRLeafNode(
    leaf_id="skills/retail-policy/SKILL.md#Return-Window",
    file_path="skills/retail-policy/SKILL.md",
    breadcrumb="skills > retail-policy > Return Window",
    content="Standard items have a 30-day return window.",
)
node2 = STAIRLeafNode(
    leaf_id="skills/retail-policy/SKILL.md#Refund-Policy",
    file_path="skills/retail-policy/SKILL.md",
    breadcrumb="skills > retail-policy > Refund Policy",
    content="Refunds are processed within 5 business days.",
)
trie.insert(node1.leaf_id, node1)
trie.insert(node2.leaf_id, node2)

assert trie.resolve_exact("skills/retail-policy/SKILL.md#Return-Window") ==
node1
assert trie.resolve_exact("skills/retail-policy/SKILL.md#Refund-Policy") ==
node2
assert trie.resolve_exact("skills/retail-policy/SKILL.md#Non-Existent") is None
assert "s" in trie.allowed_next_chars("")
assert "#" in trie.allowed_next_chars("skills/retail-policy/SKILL.md")
assert trie.allowed_next_chars("phantom/path") == []
print("  ✓ STAIRPrefixTrie (Insertion, Masking, Exact Resolution): VERIFIED")

# Test B: HermesSTAIRVerifier Workspace ToC Indexing (LN_D)
verifier = HermesSTAIRVerifier(Path("'"$WORKSPACE"'"))
indexed_count = verifier.build_icm_toc_index()
assert indexed_count >= 50, f"Expected >= 50 indexed leaves, got
{indexed_count}"
print(f"  ✓ HermesSTAIRVerifier ToC Indexing ({indexed_count} Leaf Nodes):
VERIFIED")

# Test C: Two-Stage MRL Retrieval (256d coarse -> 768d fine)
leaves = verifier.retrieve_stair_leaves("How does the refund policy operate?",
top_k=3)
assert len(leaves) > 0, "MRL retrieval returned empty leaf list"
for leaf in leaves:
    assert len(leaf.embedding_256d) == 256
    assert len(leaf.embedding_768d) == 768
print("  ✓ Two-Stage MRL Retrieval (256d coarse -> 768d fine): VERIFIED")

# Test D: Pre-Execution Epistemic & Policy Grounding Gate
evidence = ["Verified user USR-4421 and active order ORD-8812."]

# Ungrounded phantom order ID: MUST fail
grounded_fail, msg_fail = verifier.verify_tool_call_grounding(
    "cancel_order", {"order_id": "ORD-9999"}, evidence
)
assert not grounded_fail, "STAIR verifier failed to block phantom order_id
ORD-9999"
assert "STAIR Epistemic Gate Violation" in msg_fail

# Grounded order ID: MUST pass
grounded_pass, msg_pass = verifier.verify_tool_call_grounding(
    "cancel_order", {"order_id": "ORD-8812"}, evidence
)
assert grounded_pass, f"STAIR verifier blocked legitimate order_id: {msg_pass}"
assert msg_pass == "STAIR_GROUNDED_PASS"
print("  ✓ Pre-Execution Epistemic & Policy Grounding Gate: VERIFIED")

# Test E: MCP Ephemeral Bridge Integration Verification
import importlib.util
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("bridge",
"'"$WORKSPACE"'/.hermes/mcp_ephemeral_bridge.py")
bridge = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(bridge)
assert hasattr(bridge, "STAIR_VERIFIER"), "Bridge missing STAIR_VERIFIER
instance"
assert isinstance(bridge.STAIR_VERIFIER, HermesSTAIRVerifier)
print("  ✓ Ephemeral FastMCP Bridge Integration (Port 1934): VERIFIED")
'

# 3. Hardware Invariant Checks
VRAM_USED=$(nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits |

python3 -c "assert float('$VRAM_USED') <= 5400.0, f'VRAM ceiling breached:
$VRAM_USED MiB'"

# 4. Execute Deterministic Fresh-Session Unit & Decision Ledger Test Suite

import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Add workspace to path
sys.path.insert(0, "'"$WORKSPACE"'")

from scripts.hermes_fresh_session_verifier import (
    FreshSessionVerifier,
    DecisionLogger,
    SystemOneGateType,
    DecisionReceipt,
)

verifier = FreshSessionVerifier("'"$WORKSPACE"'")

# Test A: History Stripping Protocol (Zero-History Isolation)
raw_messages = [
    {"role": "system", "content": "You are a customer support agent."},
    {"role": "user", "content": "Can you cancel order ORD-100?"},
    {"role": "assistant", "content": "Let me check with tool...
<deliberation>checking</deliberation>"},
    {"role": "tool", "content": "Order ORD-100 found for user USR-200 with
status PENDING."},
    {"role": "assistant", "content": "HANDOFF.md: Customer requested cancel,
status verified."},
]
clean_msgs, read_obs, cand = verifier.strip_conversational_history(raw_messages)
assert len(clean_msgs) == 2, f"Expected 2 clean messages, got {len(clean_msgs)}"
assert clean_msgs[0]["role"] == "system"
assert clean_msgs[1]["role"] == "tool"
assert len(read_obs) == 1
assert "ORD-100" in read_obs[0]
print("  ✓ History Stripping Protocol (HANDOFF.md / Chat chatter stripped):
VERIFIED")

# Test B: Gabriel Azoulay & Andrew Carter "Three Hats" Verification
spec = "def cancel_pending_order(order_id: str) -> bool: Cancel pending order."
evidence = "Order ORD-100 status PENDING, user USR-200 verified."

# Hat 1/2/3 Valid Action Test
pass_res = verifier.evaluate_three_hats(spec, evidence, {"order_id": "ORD-100"})
assert pass_res["overall_passed"] is True
assert pass_res["hat_1_spec"]["passed"] is True
assert pass_res["hat_2_evidence"]["passed"] is True
assert pass_res["hat_3_policy"]["passed"] is True

# Hat 2 Grounding Failure (Phantom ID)
fail_grounding = verifier.evaluate_three_hats(spec, evidence, {"order_id":
"ORD-9999"})
assert fail_grounding["overall_passed"] is False
assert fail_grounding["hat_2_evidence"]["passed"] is False

# Hat 3 Policy Failure (Forbidden sycophancy)
fail_policy = verifier.evaluate_three_hats(spec, evidence, "As an AI assistant,
I do not have access.")
assert fail_policy["overall_passed"] is False
assert fail_policy["hat_3_policy"]["passed"] is False
print("  ✓ One Reader in Three Hats Protocol (Spec, Evidence, Policy):
VERIFIED")

# Test C: Append-Only Decision Receipts Ledger & Six System One Gates
gates = [
    SystemOneGateType.INTENT_GUARD,
    SystemOneGateType.RULE_GUARD,
    SystemOneGateType.SCOPE_GUARD,
    SystemOneGateType.DONE_GATE,
    SystemOneGateType.CLAIMS_GATE,
    SystemOneGateType.COMMIT_GUARD,
]
for g in gates:
    ok, summary, rcpt = verifier.evaluate_and_log(
        g, spec, evidence, {"order_id": "ORD-100"}, metadata={"gate_test":
g.value}
    )
    assert ok is True
    assert len(rcpt.candidate_snapshot_hash) == 64  # SHA-256 length

receipts = verifier.decision_logger.query_receipts(limit=10)
assert len(receipts) >= 6
print(f"  ✓ Six System One Gates & Append-Only Decision Ledger ({len(receipts)}
receipts): VERIFIED")

# Test D: Ephemeral Bridge Integration Verification
import importlib.util
spec_mod = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("bridge",
"'"$WORKSPACE"'/.hermes/mcp_ephemeral_bridge.py")
bridge = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec_mod)
spec_mod.loader.exec_module(bridge)
assert hasattr(bridge, "FRESH_VERIFIER"), "Bridge missing FRESH_VERIFIER
instance"
assert isinstance(bridge.FRESH_VERIFIER, FreshSessionVerifier)
print("  ✓ FastMCP Ephemeral Bridge Decision Gate Integration (Port 1934):
VERIFIED")

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo "=== Phase 53 Verification Contract: Zero-Bespoke Upstream Consolidation
==="

REPO_DIR="/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes"
CHEZMOI_DIR="$REPO_DIR/.local_archived/share/chezmoi"

# 1. Assert 0 bespoke scripts exist in scripts/ or private_dot_hermes/
echo "[1/7] Checking for bespoke scripts..."
BESPOKE_COUNT=0
for d in "$CHEZMOI_DIR/scripts" "$CHEZMOI_DIR/private_dot_hermes"
"$REPO_DIR/scripts"; do
  if [ -d "$d" ]; then
    count=$(find "$d" -maxdepth 1 \( -name "executable_*.py" -o -name
"executable_*.sh" -o -name "*.py" -o -name "*.sh" \) 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
    BESPOKE_COUNT=$((BESPOKE_COUNT + count))
  fi
done
if [ "$BESPOKE_COUNT" -ne 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Found $BESPOKE_COUNT bespoke executable scripts."
  exit 1
fi
echo "PASS: Zero bespoke scripts found in repository."

# 2. Assert no custom plugin exists
echo "[2/7] Checking for custom plugins..."
if [ -d "/var/home/imshubhamsocial/.hermes/plugins/hermes-icm-os" ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Custom plugin directory hermes-icm-os exists."
  exit 1
fi
echo "PASS: No custom plugins exist."

# 3. Assert ICM line limits on all contracts (<80 lines) and reference files
(<200 lines)
echo "[3/7] Enforcing ICM line limits..."
for f in "$REPO_DIR/AGENTS.md" "$REPO_DIR/CONTEXT.md"
"$REPO_DIR"/stages/*/CONTEXT.md; do
  lines=$(wc -l < "$f")
  if [ "$lines" -ge 80 ]; then
    echo "FAIL: $f has $lines lines (limit is < 80)"
    exit 1
  fi
done
for f in "$REPO_DIR"/rules/*.md "$REPO_DIR"/_config/*.md; do
  lines=$(wc -l < "$f")
  if [ "$lines" -ge 200 ]; then
    echo "FAIL: $f has $lines lines (limit is < 200)"
    exit 1
  fi
done
echo "PASS: All ICM contracts (<80) and reference files (<200) strictly within
limits."

# 4. Assert plugins are enabled in Hermes
echo "[4/7] Checking Hermes plugins status..."
PLUGINS_OUT=$(hermes plugins list --plain --no-bundled)
if ! echo "$PLUGINS_OUT" | grep -q "jev-curator"; then
  echo "FAIL: jev-curator plugin not enabled."
  exit 1
fi
if ! echo "$PLUGINS_OUT" | grep -q "openviking"; then
  echo "FAIL: openviking plugin not enabled."
  exit 1
fi
echo "PASS: Official catalog plugins jev-curator and openviking are enabled."

# 5. Assert Substrate Purity on .hermes/hermes-agent
echo "[5/7] Checking upstream hermes-agent git purity..."
AGENT_DIR="$REPO_DIR/.hermes/hermes-agent"
if [ -d "$AGENT_DIR/.git" ]; then
  DIRTY_COUNT=$(cd "$AGENT_DIR" && git status --porcelain | wc -l)
  if [ "$DIRTY_COUNT" -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "FAIL: Upstream hermes-agent is dirty ($DIRTY_COUNT modified files)."
    exit 1
  fi
fi
echo "PASS: Upstream hermes-agent is 100% clean."

# 6. Assert live NVML VRAM <= 5400.0 MiB
echo "[6/7] Checking NVML VRAM..."
VRAM_USED=$(nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits |
head -n1 | tr -d ' ')
if [ "$VRAM_USED" -gt 5400 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: GPU VRAM exceeds ceiling: ${VRAM_USED} MiB > 5400.0 MiB."
  exit 1
fi
echo "PASS: GPU VRAM invariant satisfied (${VRAM_USED} MiB <= 5400.0 MiB)."

# 7. Cryptographically seal verification receipt
echo "[7/7] Sealing verification receipt..."
RECEIPT_FILE="/tmp/hermes_verified_receipt.json"
cat <<EOF > "$RECEIPT_FILE"
{
  "phase": 53,
  "phase_name": "phase_53_zero_bespoke_upstream_consolidation_icm_5layer",
  "status": "PASS",
  "exitstatus": 0,
  "vram_used_mib": $VRAM_USED,
  "timestamp": "$(date -u +'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')",
  "sha256": "$(sha256sum "$REPO_DIR/AGENTS.md" | awk '{print $1}')"
}
EOF

echo "Phase 53 verified and sealed successfully."
exit 0

  CONFIG_FILE="$HOME/.hermes/config.yaml"

    echo "[PASS] $desc"

  else

  fi

01-04)" \

\

  CONFIG_FILE="$HOME/.hermes/config.yaml"

    echo "[PASS] $desc"

phases/phase_56_candidate3_q8_kv_dflash_and_icm_7gauntlet_blueprints/03_verify/

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Phase 56 Verification Contract: Candidate 3 Substrate & ICM 7-Gauntlet
Blueprints
set -euo pipefail

TOTAL=15
PASSED=0
WORKSPACE_ROOT="/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes"
cd "$WORKSPACE_ROOT"

echo "=== Running Phase 56 Verification Contract ==="

# 1. Upstream Submodule Purity
UPSTREAM_DIFF="$(git -C .hermes/hermes-agent status --porcelain 2>/dev/null ||
true)"
if [[ -z "$UPSTREAM_DIFF" ]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 1/$TOTAL: Upstream substrate .hermes/hermes-agent is 100% clean"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 1/$TOTAL: Upstream substrate .hermes/hermes-agent contains
uncommitted modifications"
  exit 1
fi

# 2. Discrete RTX 3050 VRAM Ceiling Check (<= 5800.0 MiB)
VRAM_USED="$(nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits |
head -n1 | tr -d ' ')"
VRAM_FLOAT="$(python3 -c "print(float('$VRAM_USED'))")"
CEILING=5800.0
if (( $(echo "$VRAM_FLOAT <= $CEILING" | bc -l) )); then
  echo "[PASS] 2/$TOTAL: NVML VRAM check: ${VRAM_FLOAT} MiB <= ${CEILING} MiB"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 2/$TOTAL: NVML VRAM check failed: ${VRAM_FLOAT} MiB exceeds
ceiling ${CEILING} MiB"
  exit 1
fi

# 3. Llama-server Port 8138 Health
LLAMA_STATUS="$(curl -s --max-time 3
[http://127.0.0.1:8138/health](http://127.0.0.1:8138/health) || echo "{}")"
if echo "$LLAMA_STATUS" | grep -q '"status":"ok"'; then
  echo "[PASS] 3/$TOTAL: llama-server Port 8138 health check passed:
$LLAMA_STATUS"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 3/$TOTAL: llama-server Port 8138 health check failed:
$LLAMA_STATUS"
  exit 1
fi

# 4. Hermes Gateway Port 8642 Health
GATEWAY_STATUS="$(curl -s --max-time 3
[http://127.0.0.1:8642/health](http://127.0.0.1:8642/health) || echo "{}")"
if echo "$GATEWAY_STATUS" | grep -q '"status": "ok"'; then
  echo "[PASS] 4/$TOTAL: Hermes Gateway Port 8642 health check passed:
$GATEWAY_STATUS"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 4/$TOTAL: Hermes Gateway Port 8642 health check failed:
$GATEWAY_STATUS"
  exit 1
fi

# 5. OpenViking Port 1933 Health
VIKING_STATUS="$(curl -s --max-time 3
[http://127.0.0.1:1933/health](http://127.0.0.1:1933/health) || echo "{}")"
if echo "$VIKING_STATUS" | grep -q '"status":"ok"'; then
  echo "[PASS] 5/$TOTAL: OpenViking Port 1933 health check passed:
$VIKING_STATUS"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 5/$TOTAL: OpenViking Port 1933 health check failed:
$VIKING_STATUS"
  exit 1
fi

# 6. Dream-RSI Pre-Gauntlet Warm-Start Distillation
RSI_RECEIPT="$WORKSPACE_ROOT/.hermes/cache/rsi_last_distill.json"
if [[ -s "$RSI_RECEIPT" ]] && python3 -m json.tool "$RSI_RECEIPT" >/dev/null
2>&1; then
  TREES_COUNT="$(python3 -c "import json; data=json.load(open('$RSI_RECEIPT'));
print(data.get('evaluated_trees', 0))")"
  echo "[PASS] 6/$TOTAL: Dream-RSI warm-start receipt verified (evaluated
$TREES_COUNT trees)"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 6/$TOTAL: Dream-RSI warm-start receipt missing or invalid"
  exit 1
fi

# 7. Verification of all 7 Gauntlet Directories
GAUNTLETS=(gauntlet_a_voxel_fps gauntlet_b_splatbox gauntlet_c_aquarium
leanrea_eval ffmpeg_skill_eval diagram_design_eval markitdown_eval)
MISSING_GAUNTLETS=0
for g in "${GAUNTLETS[@]}"; do
  if [[ ! -d "$WORKSPACE_ROOT/benchmarks/$g" ]]; then
    echo "Missing benchmark directory: benchmarks/$g"
    MISSING_GAUNTLETS=1
  fi
done
if [[ "$MISSING_GAUNTLETS" -eq 0 ]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 7/$TOTAL: All 7 frontier gauntlet directories exist in
benchmarks/"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 7/$TOTAL: Missing one or more gauntlet directories"
  exit 1
fi

# 8. Verification of all 21 Staged Prompt Files
MISSING_PROMPTS=0
for g in "${GAUNTLETS[@]}"; do
  for s in 1 2 3; do
    PFILE="$WORKSPACE_ROOT/benchmarks/$g/PROMPT_STAGE${s}.txt"
    if [[ ! -s "$PFILE" ]]; then
      echo "Missing or empty prompt: benchmarks/$g/PROMPT_STAGE${s}.txt"
      MISSING_PROMPTS=1
    fi
  done
done
if [[ "$MISSING_PROMPTS" -eq 0 ]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 8/$TOTAL: All 21 staged prompt files (3 stages * 7 gauntlets)
exist and are non-empty"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 8/$TOTAL: One or more staged prompt files missing or empty"
  exit 1
fi

# 9. Verification of all 7 Gauntlet launch_konsole.sh Scripts
MISSING_LAUNCHERS=0
for g in "${GAUNTLETS[@]}"; do
  LFILE="$WORKSPACE_ROOT/benchmarks/$g/launch_konsole.sh"
  if [[ ! -x "$LFILE" ]] || ! grep -q "PROMPT_STAGE" "$LFILE"; then
    echo "Invalid or non-executable launcher: benchmarks/$g/launch_konsole.sh"
    MISSING_LAUNCHERS=1
  fi
done
if [[ "$MISSING_LAUNCHERS" -eq 0 ]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 9/$TOTAL: All 7 gauntlet launch_konsole.sh scripts are executable
and stage-aware"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 9/$TOTAL: One or more launch_konsole.sh scripts invalid"
  exit 1
fi

# 10. Verification of Master launch_all_konsole.sh
if [[ -x "$WORKSPACE_ROOT/benchmarks/launch_all_konsole.sh" ]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 10/$TOTAL: Master benchmarks/launch_all_konsole.sh is executable"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 10/$TOTAL: Master benchmarks/launch_all_konsole.sh missing or
non-executable"
  exit 1
fi

# 11. Tripartite Markdown Line Budget Discipline across All 7 Gauntlets
LINE_CAP_FAIL=0
for g in "${GAUNTLETS[@]}"; do
  # CONTEXT <= 80, invariants <= 80, rules <= 120, references <= 200
  C_LINES=$(wc -l < "$WORKSPACE_ROOT/benchmarks/$g/CONTEXT.md")
  I_LINES=$(wc -l < "$WORKSPACE_ROOT/benchmarks/$g/invariants.md")
  R_LINES=$(wc -l < "$WORKSPACE_ROOT/benchmarks/$g/rules.md")
  REF_LINES=$(wc -l < "$WORKSPACE_ROOT/benchmarks/$g/references.md")
  if (( C_LINES > 80 || I_LINES > 80 || R_LINES > 120 || REF_LINES > 200 ));
then
    echo "Line cap violated in benchmarks/$g: C=$C_LINES, I=$I_LINES,
R=$R_LINES, REF=$REF_LINES"
    LINE_CAP_FAIL=1
  fi
done
if [[ "$LINE_CAP_FAIL" -eq 0 ]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 11/$TOTAL: All 7 gauntlet markdown files strictly obey ICM line
budgets (<= 80/80/120/200)"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 11/$TOTAL: ICM line budgets violated in gauntlet markdown files"
  exit 1
fi

# 12. Verification of Preloaded Skills in ~/.hermes/skills/
REQUIRED_SKILLS=(icm-architect leanrea ffmpeg-skill diagram-design markitdown)
SKILL_FAIL=0
for sk in "${REQUIRED_SKILLS[@]}"; do
  if [[ ! -d "$HOME/.hermes/skills/$sk" ]] && [[ ! -d
"/var/home/imshubhamsocial/.hermes/skills/$sk" ]]; then
    echo "Required skill missing: $sk"
    SKILL_FAIL=1
  fi
done
if [[ "$SKILL_FAIL" -eq 0 ]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 12/$TOTAL: All 5 canonical skills are installed in
~/.hermes/skills/"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 12/$TOTAL: Missing one or more required skills in
~/.hermes/skills/"
  exit 1
fi

# 13. Candidate 3 Model Weights Verification
MODEL_MAIN="/var/home/imshubhamsocial/.hermes/models/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-
E4B-UD-Q4_K_XL.gguf"
MODEL_DRAFT="/var/home/imshubhamsocial/.hermes/models/dflash-NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5
-Bolt-E4B-Q8_0.gguf"
if [[ ! -f "$MODEL_MAIN" ]]; then
  MODEL_MAIN="$HOME/.hermes/models/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-E4B-UD-Q4_K_XL.gguf"
fi
if [[ ! -f "$MODEL_DRAFT" ]]; then

MODEL_DRAFT="$HOME/.hermes/models/dflash-NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-E4B-Q8_0.gguf"
fi
if [[ -f "$MODEL_MAIN" ]] && [[ -f "$MODEL_DRAFT" ]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 13/$TOTAL: Candidate 3 target model and DFlash drafter GGUF
weights verified"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 13/$TOTAL: Candidate 3 model weights missing from
~/.hermes/models/"
  exit 1
fi

# 14. Bespoke Script Purity Gate
BESPOKE_COUNT=0
if [[ -d "$WORKSPACE_ROOT/scripts" ]]; then
  for f in "$WORKSPACE_ROOT/scripts"/*; do
    [[ -f "$f" ]] || continue
    fname="$(basename "$f")"
    if [[ "$fname" != "hermes_icm_validator.py" ]] && [[ "$fname" !=
"rule_icm_validation_script_missing.md" ]]; then
      ((BESPOKE_COUNT++))
    fi
  done
fi
if [[ "$BESPOKE_COUNT" -eq 0 ]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 14/$TOTAL: Zero bespoke scripts in scripts/ (canonical substrate
preserved)"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 14/$TOTAL: Detected $BESPOKE_COUNT unauthorized bespoke scripts
in scripts/"
  exit 1
fi

# 15. Tripartite Scaffolding for Phase 56
if [[ -s
"$WORKSPACE_ROOT/phases/
phase_56_candidate3_q8_kv_dflash_and_icm_7gauntlet_blueprints/01_spec/spec.md"
]] && \
   [[ -s
"$WORKSPACE_ROOT/phases/
phase_56_candidate3_q8_kv_dflash_and_icm_7gauntlet_blueprints/02_impl/task.md"
]]; then
  echo "[PASS] 15/$TOTAL: Phase 56 tripartite spec and impl markdown files
verified"
  PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))
else
  echo "[FAIL] 15/$TOTAL: Phase 56 spec or impl files missing or empty"
  exit 1

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Phase 58 Deterministic Verification Contract (GDR36 Master Certification)

Purity..."

-q "^" && exit 1 || true

echo "[Phase 58] 2/7 Executing 8-Point ICM Walk Test & Static Link Validator..."
python3 _tools/hermes_icm_validator.py

echo "[Phase 58] 3/7 Verifying icm-architect Skill Registration..."
test -f skills/icm-architect/SKILL.md

echo "[Phase 58] 4/7 Auditing Gauntlet A (3D Voxel FPS & 20 Hz Netcode)..."
bash benchmarks/gauntlet_a_voxel_fps/contract.sh

echo "[Phase 58] 5/7 Auditing Gauntlet B (3-Stage ICM Splatbox CV Pipeline)..."
python3 _tools/hermes_stage_runner.py --stage-dir stages/01-probe --gate-mode
autonomous --verify-only
python3 _tools/hermes_stage_runner.py --stage-dir stages/02-controller
--gate-mode autonomous --verify-only
python3 _tools/hermes_stage_runner.py --stage-dir stages/03-execute --gate-mode
autonomous --verify-only
bash benchmarks/gauntlet_b_splatbox/contract.sh

echo "[Phase 58] 6/7 Auditing Gauntlet C (3D WebGL Aquarium & Buoyancy)..."
bash benchmarks/gauntlet_c_aquarium/contract.sh

echo "[Phase 58] 7/7 Verifying RTX 3050 Hardware Quiescence & Ledger
Integrity..."
if systemctl --user is-active --quiet hermes-llama-server.service; then
    python3 _tools/check_vram_limit.py --mode active --max-active 5800.0
else
    python3 _tools/check_vram_limit.py --mode quiescent --max-quiescent 100.0
fi
python3 -m json.tool phases/status_ledger.json > /dev/null

echo "[PASS] Phase 58 Autonomous ICM Software Factory & Multi-Gauntlet Suite
Certified (Exit 0)."

```python
import zipfile, json

zf = zipfile.ZipFile('./imshubhamsocial/ws-hermes', 'r')
prefix = zf.namelist()[0].split('/')[0] + '/'

def read_file(rel_path):
    return zf.read(prefix + rel_path).decode('utf-8', errors='replace')

print("=== status_ledger.json ===")
ledger = read_file('phases/status_ledger.json')
print(ledger[:2500])
print("...\n", ledger[-2500:])

print("\n=== .config and .local and .openviking and .hermes top-level ===")
for p in ['.config/systemd/user/hermes-llama-server.service',
          '.config/systemd/user/hermes-openviking.service',
          '.hermes/config.yaml',
          '_tools/index.py', '_tools/links.py', '_tools/quotes.py',
'_tools/viz.py']:
    print("="*60, p)
    print(read_file(p)[:1000])

[Unit]
Description=Hermes Local LLM Inference Daemon (llama-server)
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
ExecStart=%h/.local/bin/llama-server
Restart=always
RestartSec=5s
KillMode=process
LimitNOFILE=65535

[Install]
WantedBy=default.target

============================================================ _tools/index.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Write _index.md in each folder named: one line per note, built from the note
itself.

    python -I _tools/index.py FOLDER [FOLDER ...]

FOLDER is relative to the current folder or to the workspace root (the folder
that holds _tools/);
a folder that does not exist is skipped. A note is a .md file in the folder, or
a subfolder with a
CONTEXT.md (a record). Each line is  - [title](file) - description (status) .
The title falls back
to the first heading, then the file name; description and status appear when the
frontmatter has
them. Rerun after adding or changing notes; never edit _index.md by hand.
Standard library only.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""List every relative link and input path that does not resolve. Exit 1 if

Copy to _tools/ in a workspace and run from anywhere:
    python -I _tools/links.py [ROOT] [--skip FOLDER ...] [--graph FILE]
ROOT defaults to the folder that holds _tools/. Standard library only.

Checks every .md file outside dot-folders, node_modules/, _archive/,
_templates/, raw/ and any
folder named with --skip:
  - markdown links [text](path), .md paths in consumes: / produces: frontmatter,
and import

hold).
    Web links, #anchors, {placeholders} and [[wikilinks]] are not checked.
  - backticked paths in list items under an Inputs heading, relative to the file
or to ROOT.

folder exists,
    since this run's output may not be made yet.

============================================================ _tools/quotes.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3

that nothing on the

    python -I _tools/quotes.py [ROOT] [--show-cuts]
ROOT defaults to the folder that holds _tools/. Standard library only.

A quote is  "verbatim line" (key position)  where
  key       names a file in sources/raw/ (its name without the extension, or a
short name
            declared in _meta/sources.txt as  key = filename)
  position  a timestamp or range for transcripts (12:30, 1:02:15, 12:30-13:05),
or a page or
            page range for documents (page 2, p2, pp 4-5)
Transcripts are timed when their lines start with **** or. A document is paged
when
it has lines that read  Page 1, Page 2 ... ; any other source is matched
anywhere and its position
is not checked. A cut inside a quote is w
============================================================ _tools/viz.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3

internet.
- A node is a .md file; catalogs and logs (AGENTS.md and its pointers, index.md,

  log.md) are left out because they link to everything.
- Colors follow the frontmatter type, most common type first. The first node
shown is the

import zipfile

zf = zipfile.ZipFile('./imshubhamsocial/ws-hermes', 'r')
prefix = zf.namelist()[0].split('/')[0] + '/'

rel_names = [n[len(prefix):] for n in zf.namelist() if n[len(prefix):]]

# List top-level subdirectories of .hermes/skills/
skill_entries = sorted(set(
    '/'.join(n.split('/')[:3]) for n in rel_names if
n.startswith('.hermes/skills/')
))
print("=== .hermes/skills entries ===")
for s in skill_entries:
    print(s)

# Check .hermes/state* files
for p in ['.hermes/spawn-ledger.json', '.hermes/state-db', '.hermes/state_db',
'.hermes/statedb',
          '.local/bin/llama-server', '.local/bin/openviking-server',
'.openviking/ov.conf', '.openviking/ovcli.conf']:
    info = zf.getinfo(prefix + p)
    content = zf.read(prefix + p)
    print(f"=== {p} (size={info.file_size},
external_attr={hex(info.external_attr)}) ===")
    print(content[:500].decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))
