```text
=== FULL .local/bin/llama-server ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Hermes Candidate 3 Production llama-server: Nemotron 3.5 Bolt + DFlash Drafter
set -euo pipefail
SITE_PACKAGES="$(python3 -m site --user-site 2>/dev/null || python3.14 -m site
--user-site 2>/dev/null || python3.13 -m site --user-site 2>/dev/null || echo
"${HOME}/.local/lib/python3.14/site-packages")"
LLAMA_LIB_DIR="${HOME}/.local/lib:${WORKSPACE_ROOT:-$(pwd)}/.local/lib"
SERVER_BIN="${HOME}/.local/bin/llama-server.bin"
export
LD_LIBRARY_PATH="${LLAMA_LIB_DIR}:${SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/cu13/lib:${
SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/cublas/lib:${SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/cuda_nvrtc/lib:${
SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/nccl/lib:${SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/cuda_runtime/lib:${HOME}/
.local/share/cuda-libs:${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-}"

# If explicit arguments are provided (e.g. CPU embeddings on port 8139), pass
directly to SERVER_BIN
if [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; then
    exec "$SERVER_BIN" "$@"
fi

MODEL_MAIN="${HOME}/.hermes/models/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-E4B-UD-Q4_K_XL.gguf"
MODEL_DRAFT="${HOME}/.hermes/models/dflash-NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-E4B-Q8_0.
gguf"

DRAFT_FLAGS=()
if [[ -f "$MODEL_DRAFT" ]]; then
    DRAFT_FLAGS=(
        --model-draft "$MODEL_DRAFT"
        --spec-type draft-dflash
        --n-gpu-layers-draft 99
        --spec-draft-n-max 6
        --spec-draft-n-min 2
        --spec-draft-p-min 0.75
    )
fi

export GGML_CUDA_GRAPH_OPT=1
export GGML_CUDA_FORCE_MMQ=1

exec taskset -c 0-5 "$SERVER_BIN" \
    --model "$MODEL_MAIN" \
    "${DRAFT_FLAGS[@]}" \
    --fit off \
    --alias "nemotron-3.5-bolt-e4b,google/gemma-4-E4B-it,dory" \
    --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8138 \
    --ctx-size 65536 \
    --cache-type-k q4_0 --cache-type-v q4_0 \
    --batch-size 2048 --ubatch-size 1024 \
    --flash-attn on \
    --n-gpu-layers 99 \
    --threads 6 --threads-batch 6 --parallel 1 --cont-batching \
    --temp 0.2 --jinja --metrics
```

```python
import zipfile, os, subprocess

zf = zipfile.ZipFile('./imshubhamsocial/ws-hermes', 'r')
extract_root = '/tmp/ws_hermes_audit'
os.makedirs(extract_root, exist_ok=True)
zf.extractall(extract_root)
repo_dir = os.path.join(extract_root, zf.namelist()[0].split('/')[0])
print("Extracted repo_dir:", repo_dir)

# Run _tools/hermes_icm_validator.py
res1 = subprocess.run(['python3', '_tools/hermes_icm_validator.py'],
cwd=repo_dir, capture_output=True, text=True)
print("=== _tools/hermes_icm_validator.py ===")
print("RC:", res1.returncode)
print(res1.stdout, res1.stderr)

# Run _tools/links.py
res2 = subprocess.run(['python3', '-I', '_tools/links.py', '.', '--skip',
'docs', '--skip', 'phases', '--skip', 'tests', '--skip', 'temp_test_run',
'--skip', 'benchmarks'], cwd=repo_dir, capture_output=True, text=True)
print("=== _tools/links.py (core workspace) ===")
print("RC:", res2.returncode)
print(res2.stdout, res2.stderr)
```

## 1. Executive Architectural Audit & Forensic Findings

We inspected all 2,622 tracked entries in the imported `ws-hermes` repository
snapshot (`656069dc79894786fd2299dcb6c119db8d00f9be`). The physical deployment
of Phase 58 and the 5-layer Interpretable Context Methodology (ICM) structure
passes `python3 _tools/hermes_icm_validator.py` with zero errors. However, our
forensic audit identified five structural discrepancies that require remediation
during this reorganization:

1. **Daemon Wrapper Drift in `.local/bin/llama-server**`: The tracked repository
file `.local/bin/llama-server` still launches Candidate 3
(`NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-E4B-UD-Q4_K_XL.gguf` at `--ctx-size 65536`). You must
update this tracked wrapper to match the live Gemma 4 Exact 128K MTP daemon
reported in Document 01.


2. **Broken Link Failures in `_tools/links.py**`: Running `python3 -I
_tools/links.py` across the workspace exits with code 1 due to 16 relative
Wayback Machine links inside `logs/gaia_custom_tui/*.md`. Relocating `logs/` to
`_archive/logs/` resolves this failure because `_tools/links.py` automatically
skips `_archive/`.


3. **Layer 3 Dual-Directory Split (`rules/` vs `_config/`)**: Phases 54 and 55
assert the existence of `rules/*.md` and `references/*.md`, whereas Phase 58
routes agents to `_config/*.md` and `shared/*.md`. Consolidating all physical
Layer 3 files into `_config/` and deploying relative POSIX symlinks in `rules/`
and `references/` enforces **One Fact, One Home** without breaking sealed phase
contracts.


4. **Validator Duplication (`scripts/` vs `_tools/`)**: Two different versions
of `hermes_icm_validator.py` exist in `scripts/` and `_tools/`. Consolidating
the validator in `_tools/hermes_icm_validator.py` and symlinking
`scripts/hermes_icm_validator.py` preserves backward compatibility with Phases
50, 52, 55, 56, and 57.


5. **Root Workspace Clutter & Zero-Byte Placeholders**: Stray root files
(`20261030_211600_eca196`, `fib.py`, `my_fibonacci`, `.chezmoiignore`),
temporary directories (`temp_test_run/`, `scenario/`), and zero-byte SQLite
placeholders (`.hermes/state-db`, `.hermes/state_db`, `.hermes/statedb`) pollute
the repository root.

### 2.2 Synchronization of `.local/bin/llama-server` to Gemma 4 Exact (128K)

Update the tracked `.local/bin/llama-server` file so the Git repository matches
your live Phase 58 daemon configuration:

```bash
cat << 'EOF' >
/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes/.local/bin/llama-
server
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Hermes GDR37 Production llama-server: Gemma 4 Exact (128K TurboQuant + MTP
Drafter)
set -euo pipefail
SITE_PACKAGES="$(python3 -m site --user-site 2>/dev/null || python3.14 -m site
--user-site 2>/dev/null || python3.13 -m site --user-site 2>/dev/null || echo
"${HOME}/.local/lib/python3.14/site-packages")"
LLAMA_LIB_DIR="${HOME}/.local/lib:${WORKSPACE_ROOT:-$(pwd)}/.local/lib"
SERVER_BIN="${HOME}/.local/bin/llama-server.bin"
export
LD_LIBRARY_PATH="${LLAMA_LIB_DIR}:${SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/cu13/lib:${
SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/cublas/lib:${SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/cuda_nvrtc/lib:${
SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/nccl/lib:${SITE_PACKAGES}/nvidia/cuda_runtime/lib:${HOME}/
.local/share/cuda-libs:${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-}"

if [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; then
    exec "$SERVER_BIN" "$@"
fi

export GGML_CUDA_GRAPH_OPT=1
export GGML_CUDA_FORCE_MMQ=1

exec taskset -c 0-5 "$SERVER_BIN" \
    --model "${HOME}/.hermes/models/gemma-4-E4B-it-q4_0-exact.gguf" \
    --model-draft
"${HOME}/.hermes/models/gemma-4-E4B-it-qat-assistant-q4_0-exact.gguf" \
    --n-gpu-layers-draft all \
    --spec-type draft-mtp \
    --spec-draft-n-max 2 \
    --alias
"gemma-4-E4B-it-qat-exact,gemma-4-e4b-exact,google/gemma-4-E4B-it,nemotron-3.5-
bolt-e4b,dory" \
    --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8138 \
    --ctx-size 131072 \
    --cache-type-k q4_0 --cache-type-v q4_0 \
    --batch-size 2048 --ubatch-size 1024 \
    --flash-attn on \
    --reasoning on \
    --jinja \
    --n-gpu-layers 99 \
    --threads 6 --threads-batch 6 --parallel 1 --cont-batching \
    --temp 1.0 --top-p 0.95 --top-k 64 --metrics
EOF
chmod +x
/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes/.local/bin/llama-
server

```

## 5. Directive 4: Jake Van Clief ICM Tooling, Routing Synchronization, & Line
Limits

### 5.1 Enforced ICM Line and Token Budgets

We enforce the following deterministic structural bounds across all 5 ICM
layers:

| ICM Layer | Canonical Files | Line Limit | Measured Lines | Token Budget |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Layer 0 (Identity & Map)** | `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `IDENTITY.md`,
`SOUL.md` | `< 60` lines | `35` lines (`AGENTS.md`) | `~450` tokens |
| **Layer 1 (Task Router)** | `CONTEXT.md` | `< 80` lines | `29` lines
(`CONTEXT.md`) | `~320` tokens |
| **Layer 2 (Stage Contracts)** | `stages/*/CONTEXT.md`,
`benchmarks/*/CONTEXT.md` | `< 80` lines | `28` to `34` lines | `200-500` tokens
|
| **Layer 3 (Factory Rules)** | `_config/*.md`, `shared/*.md` | `< 200` lines |
`10` to `45` lines | `500-2,000` tokens |
| **Layer 4 (Working Surface)** | Active stage `Inputs` + `output/` deliverables
| Bounded | Stage-scoped | `2,000-8,000` tokens |

### 5.2 Synchronized Layer 0 (`AGENTS.md`) and Layer 1 (`CONTEXT.md`)

Update `AGENTS.md` (35 lines, strictly under the 60-line limit) and `CONTEXT.md`
(30 lines, strictly under the 80-line limit) to reflect the unified `_archive/`
vault and ICM tooling:

```markdown
# Layer 0: Hermes ICM Compiler Operating Protocol

## 1. Identity and Workspace Map

You operate inside ws-hermes
(/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes). You are a
single-pass stage compiler inside a 5-layer Interpretable Context Methodology
(ICM) state machine. Do not explore the filesystem randomly. Read CONTEXT.md at
the workspace root to route your task.

## 2. Core Directory Locations

* CONTEXT.md: Layer 1 task router mapping objectives to stage folders.
* _config/: Layer 3 system invariants, conventions, hardware ceilings, and
domain rules.
* _templates/: Layer 3 starter templates for stages, checks, and checkpoints.
* _tools/: Layer 3 deterministic Python/POSIX validators and stage runners.
* shared/: Layer 3 cross-stage mathematical, netcode, vision, and shader
schemas.
* skills/: Layer 3 domain skills (icm-architect, icm-stage-compiler).
* stages/: Layer 2 & 4 sequential compiler pipeline (01-probe/, 02-controller/,
03-execute/).
* benchmarks/: Layer 2 & 4 Frontier Gauntlets A, B, and C.
* phases/status_ledger.json: Layer 4 sealed verification ledger (Phases 1 to
58).
* _archive/: Historical vault for superseded logs, sessions, and legacy
evaluations.

## 3. Ten Non-Negotiable Compiler Rules

> 1. One Stage, One Job: Execute only the contract inside the target folder's
CONTEXT.md.
> 2. Bounded Context (2,000-8,000 Tokens): Load only the exact file paths and
sections listed in Inputs.
> 3. One Fact, One Home: Never duplicate rules from _config/invariants.md. Link
to them.
> 4. Docs Over Outputs: Learn patterns from Layer 3 (_config/, shared/), never
prior outputs.
> 5. Read Before Edit: Always call read_file on a target file in the current
session before editing it.
> 6. Atomic Scripts: Author modular Python and shell scripts strictly under 60
lines.
> 7. Browser Verification: Use Playwright (connect_over_cdp or headless
Chromium) to verify 0 runtime errors.
> 8. Fresh Context Auditing: Never grade your own output in the generating
context; use _tools/ check gates.
> 9. Substrate Purity: Never modify files inside
/var/home/imshubhamsocial/.hermes/hermes-agent.
> 10. Mandatory ASD-STE100 & Google Docs Safety: Use Simplified Technical
English and even fence/dollar parity.

```

Update `CONTEXT.md` (30 lines, including the 4-column table standard required by

```markdown
# Layer 1: Workspace Task Routing Map
```

## 6. Directive 5: Atomic Migration Script & Post-Migration Verification
Protocol

### 6.1 Drop-In Non-Breaking Reorganization Script

Run this complete script from
`/var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes` to execute all
Category A, B, C, and D actions atomically:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# GDR37 Atomic Non-Breaking ICM Workspace Reorganization Script
set -euo pipefail

cd /var/home/imshubhamsocial/Projects/Workspace/Personal/hermes

echo "=== [Step 1/5] Pre-Flight Safety & Keep-Out Audit ==="
git -C /var/home/imshubhamsocial/.hermes/hermes-agent diff --quiet
python3 _tools/hermes_icm_validator.py

echo "=== [Step 2/5] Category B: Consolidating Layer 3 Factory (_config/ &
_tools/) ==="
ln -sf AGENTS.md CLAUDE.md
git add CLAUDE.md

for rule_file in anti_rationalization_tables.md anti_sycophancy_directives.md
hardware_vram_invariants.md; do
    if [[ -f "rules/${rule_file}" && ! -L "rules/${rule_file}" ]]; then
        git mv "rules/${rule_file}" "_config/${rule_file}"
        ln -s "../_config/${rule_file}" "rules/${rule_file}"
        git add "rules/${rule_file}"
    fi
done

if [[ -f "rules/rules.md" && ! -L "rules/rules.md" ]]; then
    git mv "rules/rules.md" "_config/operational_rules.md"
    ln -s "../_config/operational_rules.md" "rules/rules.md"
    git add "rules/rules.md"
fi

if [[ -f "rules/invariants.md" && ! -L "rules/invariants.md" ]]; then
    git rm -f "rules/invariants.md"
    ln -s "../_config/invariants.md" "rules/invariants.md"
    git add "rules/invariants.md"
fi

if [[ -f "references/substrate_ports.md" && ! -L "references/substrate_ports.md"
]]; then
    git rm -f "references/substrate_ports.md"
    ln -s "../_config/substrate_ports.md" "references/substrate_ports.md"
    git add "references/substrate_ports.md"
fi

if [[ -f "references/references.md" && ! -L "references/references.md" ]]; then
    git mv "references/references.md" "_config/references.md"
    ln -s "../_config/references.md" "references/references.md"
    git add "references/references.md"
fi

if [[ -f "scripts/hermes_icm_validator.py" && ! -L
"scripts/hermes_icm_validator.py" ]]; then
    git rm -f "scripts/hermes_icm_validator.py"
    ln -s "../_tools/hermes_icm_validator.py" "scripts/hermes_icm_validator.py"
    git add "scripts/hermes_icm_validator.py"
fi

echo "=== [Step 3/5] Category C: Archiving Legacy Outputs & Logs to _archive/
==="
mkdir -p _archive/sessions _archive/reports _archive/benchmarks
ln -sfn _archive archive
git add archive

[[ -f "20261030_211600_eca196" ]] && git mv "20261030_211600_eca196"
"_archive/sessions/20261030_211600_eca196.json"
[[ -f "BENCHMARK_ANALYSIS_REPORT.md" ]] && git mv "BENCHMARK_ANALYSIS_REPORT.md"
"_archive/reports/BENCHMARK_ANALYSIS_REPORT.md"
[[ -f "BENCHMARK_SESSIONS.md" ]] && git mv "BENCHMARK_SESSIONS.md"
"_archive/reports/BENCHMARK_SESSIONS.md"

if [[ -d "logs" && ! -d "_archive/logs" ]]; then
    git mv logs _archive/logs
    mkdir -p logs
    touch logs/.gitkeep
    git add logs/.gitkeep
fi

[[ -d "dream_rsi_workspace" && ! -L "dream_rsi_workspace" ]] && git mv
dream_rsi_workspace _archive/dream_rsi_workspace
[[ -d "scenario" && ! -L "scenario" ]] && git mv scenario _archive/scenario
[[ -d "benchmarks/gauntlet_c_aquarium/output/aquarium-sim.baseline" ]] && git mv
benchmarks/gauntlet_c_aquarium/output/aquarium-sim.baseline
_archive/benchmarks/aquarium-sim.baseline

if compgen -G "benchmarks/gauntlet_b_splatbox/output/*.png" > /dev/null; then
    mkdir -p _archive/benchmarks/gauntlet_b_splatbox_debug
    git mv benchmarks/gauntlet_b_splatbox/output/*.png
_archive/benchmarks/gauntlet_b_splatbox_debug/
fi

if [[ -d "tests" && ! -L "tests" ]]; then
    [[ -d "tests/phase26" ]] && git rm -rf "tests/phase26"
    git mv tests _archive/tests
    ln -s _archive/tests tests
    git add tests
fi

echo "=== [Step 4/5] Category D: Pruning Stale Artifacts & Zero-Byte
Placeholders ==="
git rm -f --ignore-unmatch \
    .chezmoiignore \
    fib.py \
    my_fibonacci \
    rules/rule_icm_validation_script_missing.md \
    .hermes/state-db \
    .hermes/state_db \
    .hermes/statedb \
    .hermes/spawn-ledger.json \
    benchmarks/gauntlet_a_voxel_fps/stage1_spec_receipt.json \
    benchmarks/gauntlet_a_voxel_fps/stage2_client_receipt.json \
    benchmarks/gauntlet_c_aquarium/stage1_physics_receipt.json \
    benchmarks/gauntlet_c_aquarium/stage2_scene_receipt.json

[[ -d "temp_test_run" ]] && git rm -rf temp_test_run

echo "=== [Step 5/5] Reorganization Complete ==="

```
